### [1] [Exact solution of the two-dimensional one-component plasma on the truncated Beltrami pseudosphere](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21652)
*Josep Batle*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 在负曲率截断环面（Beltrami 伪球面）上，作者精确求解了耦合强度 Γ=2 的二维单组分等离子体，将配分函数化为不完全伽玛函数乘积，密度与关联函数均可显式写出，并验证了大 N 展开与共形反常——欧拉示性数为零——的预言逐项吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前二维单组分等离子体的精确解多限于平直几何（如圆柱），而弯曲表面的精确可解情形极为稀缺。作者希望在一个具有非零而相互抵消的高斯曲率与边界曲率的曲面上检验共形反常对自由能有限尺寸展开的预言（特别是欧拉示性数为零时无 ln N 项），从而为弯曲几何与边界层行为提供可解析的基准。

Method: 将截断 Beltrami 伪球面通过共形映射映到平直圆柱，使粒子间相互作用简化为平直圆柱势，而曲率仅通过面积元进入；由此配分函数化为不完全伽玛函数的乘积，密度有显式表达式，所有关联函数为行列式型。由于面积有限，在固定形状下取大 N 极限，逐项展开自由能并与 Shen–Forrester 的圆柱结果及共形反常预言对比，并用蒙特卡罗模拟（Γ=1,4,6）检验边界层随耦合的演化。

Result: 自由能大 N 展开包含体积项 (1/2)N ln N、线性项、平直硬壁表面张力乘以总边界长，以及一个依赖形状的常数项，且无 ln N 项；逐项系数与圆柱结果一致，其普适常数为两边界层的积分（quadratures）。两壁处密度均趋于平直接触值 n_b ln 2，但从相反方向逼近，尽管两圆周长相差因子 cosh u_max。渐近结果与精确解在 N 达 4096 时相符，蒙特卡罗模拟验证了边界层随耦合强度的演化。

Conclusion: 该工作首次在欧拉示性数非零但总曲率积分相消的负曲率曲面上精确求解了 Γ=2 的二维单组分等离子体，证实共形反常对自由能的预言（无 ln N 项），并给出弯曲边界层的精确行为；结果与平直圆柱逐项一致，表明普适常数由边界层决定，为曲面库仑系统与共形场论提供了精确基准。

Abstract: We solve exactly the two-dimensional one-component plasma at coupling $Γ=2$ on the truncated Beltrami pseudosphere, a surface of constant negative curvature bounded by two parallels. A conformal map onto a flat cylinder leaves the particles interacting through the flat-cylinder potential, with the curvature entering only through the area element. The partition function becomes a product of incomplete gamma functions, the density is explicit, and all correlations are determinantal. Since the area is bounded, the large-N limit is taken at fixed shape. The free energy, obtained through the constant term with every coefficient in closed form, contains a bulk term (1/2)N ln N, a term linear in N, the flat hard-wall surface tension times the total boundary length, and a shape-dependent constant, with no ln N term. This is the conformal-anomaly prediction for Euler characteristic zero, tested here on a surface where the integrated Gaussian and boundary curvatures are separately nonzero and cancel. The expansion coincides term by term with the cylinder result of Shen and Forrester, whose universal constants are quadratures of the two boundary layers. At both walls the density tends to the flat contact value $n_b$ ln 2, from opposite sides, although the circumferences differ by a factor cosh u_max. We compare the asymptotics with the exact solution up to N=4096, and Monte Carlo simulations at $Γ= $1, 4 and 6, validated against the exact $Γ= $ 2 density, show how the boundary layers evolve with coupling.

</details>


### [2] [Renormalization-Group Foundation of Critical Modes in Correlation Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21676)
*Yanhua Zhang,Mingmei Xu,Jinghua Fu,Yuanfang Wu*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 基于重正化群理论的标度算符，本文建立了一个理论框架，用以构造临界敏感涨落可观测量及其关联矩阵的完备族；关联矩阵的本征态揭示多尺度临界关联模式，其前导归一化本征值及比值呈现有限尺寸标度行为，并在二维伊辛模型中通过与自旋和能量算符关联矩阵的构造及能量扇区本征模式得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在临界现象研究中，缺乏系统性地从重正化群标度算符出发构造"临界敏感"可观测量完备族及其关联矩阵的统一理论框架，因而难以全面刻画多尺度临界关联结构与有限尺寸标度行为。

Method: 利用重正化群理论中的相关标度算符，系统构造临界敏感涨落观测量的完备族及其连通关联矩阵；通过求解关联矩阵的本征态提取多尺度临界关联模式，并考察前导归一化本征值及其比值的有限尺寸标度行为；将框架应用于二维伊辛模型，分别构造自旋与能量算符的关联矩阵并分析能量扇区的本征态模式。

Result: 在二维伊辛模型中，能量算符关联矩阵的本征态模式及前导本征值的标度行为支持了所提出的理论框架，验证了临界敏感可观测量完备族与关联矩阵的有效性，并展现出层次化的多尺度临界关联结构。

Conclusion: 所建立的框架能够系统刻画临界系统的多尺度关联结构与有限尺寸标度行为，并在二维伊辛模型中得到验证；该框架有望推广至哈密顿量未知的系统，具有广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: Based on the relevant scaling operators of renormalization-group theory, we establish a theoretical framework for constructing complete families of critical-sensitive fluctuation observables and their connected correlation matrices. The corresponding eigenstates of the correlation matrices reveal a hierarchy of multiscale critical correlation patterns and the leading normalized eigenvalues and their ratios show the finite-size scaling behavior. Applying this framework to the two-dimensional Ising model, the correlation matrices associated with both spin and energy operators are constructed. The resulting energy-sector eigenstate patterns, together with the scaling behavior of the leading eigenvalues, provide further support for the proposed framework. Finally, we discuss its potential extension to systems with unknown Hamiltonians.

</details>


### [3] [Exact Dynamic Scaling of Quantum Jump Statistics in Monitored Many-Body Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22029)
*Kazuki Yamamoto,Taiki Ishiyama,Toranosuke Matsubara,Kazuya Fujimoto,Tomohiro Sasamoto,Ryusuke Hamazaki*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了被连续监测的多体系统中子系统内量子跳跃次数的涨落，发现其具有新颖的动态标度行为：扩散时间尺度与依赖子系统尺寸的异常超泊松系统尺寸标度。对自由费米子精确求出了标度指数与标度函数，并证明在强测量极限下同样适用于相互作用费米子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测量诱导的纠缠相变等非平衡多体现象的实验观测通常需要昂贵的后选择，而量子跳跃统计为无需后选择研究此类现象提供了另一条路径，因而激发了对测量结果本身普适性的探索。

Method: 以子系统内量子跳跃次数涨落为核心可观测量，针对连续监测下的自由费米子，通过推导 Liouvillian 自关联函数的精确有限尺寸解，解析确定标度指数与标度函数；并进一步证明在大测量强度极限下，相互作用费米子具有相同的标度指数与标度函数。

Result: 发现量子跳跃次数涨落表现出扩散时间尺度与依赖子系统尺寸的异常超泊松系统尺寸标度；自由费米子情形得到精确标度指数和标度函数，且该动态标度在强测量极限下对相互作用费米子同样成立。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个新框架，通过实验友好、无需后选择的物理量来分类测量诱导的非平衡多体普适性。

Abstract: Quantum measurement has opened a new frontier in nonequilibrium many-body physics, revealing striking phenomena such as measurement-induced entanglement transitions, whose experimental observation typically requires costly postselection. Quantum jump statistics offer another route to studying such measurement-induced many-body phenomena without postselection, motivating the search for universality in measurement outcomes themselves. In this Letter, we discover that fluctuation of the number of quantum jumps within a subsystem exhibits novel dynamic scaling, characterized by diffusive time scale and anomalous super-Poissonian system-size scaling that depend on the subsystem size in monitored many-body systems. For free fermions under continuous monitoring, we exactly determine the scaling exponent and the scaling function by deriving an exact finite-size solution of the Liouvillian autocorrelation function. We also analytically show that the dynamic scaling with the same scaling exponent and the scaling function even holds for interacting fermions in the limit of large measurement strength. Our work provides a novel framework to classify measurement-induced nonequilibrium many-body universality through experimentally friendly, postselection-free quantities.

</details>


### [4] [Confinement Alters Energy Relaxation in Subrecoil Laser Cooling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22436)
*Daiki Kanbe,Eli Barkai,Takuma Akimoto*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究谐波囚禁如何改变亚反冲激光冷却中的长期能量弛豫行为。通过随机散射率模型、数值模拟与解析的更新近似，作者发现囚禁会定性地改变渐近弛豫定律：当散射率在低动量处按 R(p)∝|p|^α 趋于零时，囚禁重塑了稀有长时俘获事件的相空间几何，使低散射长寿命轨迹被限制在相空间原点附近，进而改变俘获时间统计、产生两个动力学交叉，并使 α=4 成为平均能量最快渐近衰减的最优散射率指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激光冷却需要同时降低动能并将原子约束在有限空间。已有研究多关注自由空间中的亚反冲冷却能量弛豫，但实际冷原子实验中常引入谐波囚禁势，而囚禁如何改变长期（渐近）能量弛豫规律尚未被系统阐明。作者旨在揭示囚禁与低动量散射率 R(p)∝|p|^α 的相互作用如何定性改变弛豫动力学。

Method: 采用随机散射率模型刻画冷却过程；通过数值模拟直接演化系统；并构建可解析求解的更新（renewal）近似来导出渐近弛豫定律。关键在于分析相空间中稀有、长时间低散射（俘获）事件的几何结构：自由空间中此类事件仅要求动量小，而在谐波囚禁下位置与动量沿相空间轨道耦合，长寿命低散射轨迹被限制在相空间原点附近。

Result: 囚禁定性地改变了渐近能量弛豫定律。具体表现为：(1) 相空间几何约束改变了俘获时间统计；(2) 系统出现两个动力学交叉；(3) 平均能量的渐近衰减在散射率指数 α=4 时最快，即存在最优指数 α=4。这表明即使局部散射律不变，囚禁也会改变渐近弛豫动力学。

Conclusion: 谐波囚禁通过将位置与动量耦合、改变稀有长时俘获事件的相空间几何，从而定性改变亚反冲激光冷却的长期能量弛豫定律，并给出最优散射率指数 α=4。结论强调：即便局部散射律保持不变，空间约束本身也会重塑渐近弛豫动力学。

Abstract: Controlling atomic motion through laser cooling requires both reducing kinetic energy and confining atoms to a finite spatial region. Here, we investigate how harmonic confinement modifies the long-time energy relaxation in subrecoil laser cooling. Using a stochastic scattering-rate model, numerical simulations, and an analytically tractable renewal approximation, we show that confinement qualitatively changes the asymptotic relaxation law. For a scattering rate that vanishes at low momentum as $R(p)\propto |p|^α$, the change arises because confinement reshapes the phase-space geometry of rare, long trapping events. In free space, such events require only small momentum. Under harmonic confinement, however, position and momentum are coupled along phase-space orbits, restricting long-lived low-scattering trajectories to the vicinity of the phase-space origin. This geometric constraint modifies the trapping-time statistics, produces two dynamical crossovers, and leads to an optimal scattering-rate exponent of $α=4$ for the fastest asymptotic decay of the mean energy. Our results demonstrate that confinement changes the asymptotic energy-relaxation dynamics even when the local scattering law remains unchanged.

</details>


### [5] [Bypassing the Entropy Production Speed Limit of State-to-State Transitions with Adaptive Resetting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22712)
*Daniel B. K. Chu,Rémi Goerlich,Itamar Steinitz,Yael Roichman,Barak Hirshberg*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应重置（adaptive resetting）作为一种非局域跃迁机制，可突破传统扩散驱动转变的速度-熵产基本界限，并通过混合协议与扩散-重置耦合的速度极限加以验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胶体与冷原子系统中的状态到状态转变（如势阱压缩、比特擦除）通常依赖驱动实现，其速度与熵产生受基本界限约束。作者旨在寻找超越该扩散界限的输运机制。

Method: 采用自适应重置实现非局域跃迁，其重置速率从无重置轨迹中学习；提出一种混合协议，将重置与切换到目标势阱相结合；并推导出描述扩散-重置耦合动力学的速度极限。

Result: 在三种转变过程中，自适应重置与混合协议均超越了扩散界限；并建立了耦合扩散-重置动力学的一般性速度极限。

Conclusion: 通过改变输运机制（以自适应重置替代纯扩散驱动），可以突破传统速度-熵产界限，为胶体和冷原子系统中的快速态制备与擦除提供新途径。

Abstract: State-to-state transitions in colloids and cold atoms (trap compression, bit erasure) are often achieved through driving, whose speed and entropy production are constrained by a fundamental bound. We show that this bound can be surpassed by changing the transport mechanism. Adaptive resetting enables transitions through nonlocal jumps, with rates learned from reset-free trajectories. A hybrid protocol, combining resetting with a switch to the target potential, surpasses the diffusive bound in three transformations. Finally, we derive a speed limit governing coupled diffusion-reset dynamics.

</details>


### [6] [Effects of probe size, shape, and probe-surface interactions in imaging multilayer island growth, coalescence, and formation of a smooth film](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22758)
*Ismael S. S. Carrasco,Tiago J. Oliveira,Fábio D. A. Aarão Reis*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究弱相互作用衬底上C60薄膜沉积中，AFM与XRR测得的粗糙度峰值差异问题，利用KMC模拟和探针-表面相互作用模型表明，AFM扫描模式（非接触/接触）与针尖几何形状会显著低估薄膜厚度不均匀性，差异可能源于针尖-样品卷积效应或测量间隔内的后生长过程（如岛粗化、去润湿）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前研究发现弱相互作用衬底上薄膜呈多层岛状生长，岛合并后形成光滑膜；但C60薄膜中AFM测得的粗糙度峰值明显低于XRR结果，作者旨在探究这一差异是否源于AFM的针尖-样品卷积效应及不同成像模式。

Method: 采用动力学蒙特卡洛（KMC）模拟薄膜生长，结合探针-表面相互作用模型模拟AFM成像模式。非接触模式：对表面形貌按探针宽度区域做高度平均。接触模式：使用圆柱探针及硬核相互作用模型，另比较尖锐棱锥/圆锥探针模型。并建立立方体岛生长与合并的几何模型定量验证。

Result: 非接触模式中若探针宽度L小于平均岛间距，粗糙度峰仍存在但其高度随L增大而降低，位置略有偏移；圆柱探针接触模式下峰进一步降低并显著向更小厚度移动，表现为更快的岛合并；尖锐棱锥/圆锥探针仅在L很大时才产生偏差。立方体岛几何模型定量证实了实验模拟中接触模式和针尖形状的效应。

Conclusion: SPM图像在岛合并沉积中可能低估薄膜厚度不均匀性；AFM与XRR测量结果的差异提示C60薄膜在两次测量之间可能发生了岛粗化和去润湿等后生长过程。

Abstract: Recent studies of deposition onto weakly interacting substrates have revealed multilayer island growth, followed by smooth-film formation upon island coalescence. However, for C60 films, the roughness peak measured by atomic force microscopy (AFM) was significantly lower than that obtained by X-ray reflectometry (XRR), suggesting tip-sample convolution effects. We investigate this problem using kinetic Monte Carlo (KMC) simulations of such films and models of probe-surface interactions for AFM imaging modes. To model the non-contact mode, surface profiles are transformed by averaging heights over regions matching the probe-tip width. A roughness peak remains if the probe width L is smaller than the average island distance, but its height decreases with L and its position is slightly displaced. In the contact-mode model with cylindrical probes and hard-core interactions, the peak also decreases and shifts significantly to smaller thicknesses, indicating apparently faster island coalescence. Thus, SPM images under these conditions may underestimate film-thickness nonuniformity in deposits with coalescing islands. Instead, models of sharp pyramidal or conical probes with hard-core interactions produce deviations in the roughness peaks only for large L, although such sharp tips are difficult to realize experimentally. A geometric model of cuboid island growth and coalescence quantitatively confirms the effects of contact modes and probe shapes observed in the simulations. The results suggest that post-growth processes, such as island coarsening and dewetting, may have occurred in the recently studied C60 film between the XRR and AFM measurements.

</details>


### [7] [Polymer adsorption on vicinal surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22966)
*Nathann T. Rodrigues,Tiago J. Oliveira*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 通过在近平面台阶（vicinal）表面进行大规模模拟，研究嫁接在台阶边缘的柔性聚合物链的吸附行为，发现沟槽宽度较大时存在未被预料的多临界点及三种相态（台阶附着、脱附、台面吸附），而窄沟槽下仅表现为简单的交叉过渡；台阶上的扭折不影响这些行为，并为制备聚合物纳米线提供了新思路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚合物在平面表面上的吸附已有成熟理论，粗糙表面也有一定理解，但实际中常见且实用的近平面台阶表面（vicinal surfaces）却研究甚少，存在理论空白。

Method: 对嫁接于台阶边缘（step edge）的柔性聚合物链进行大规模计算机模拟，系统考察不同台面宽度（terrace width W）及台阶扭折（kinks）条件下的吸附相行为。

Result: 当台面宽度 W ≳ 3 R_g^(2D)（R_g^(2D) 为台面吸附聚合物的回旋半径）时，体系呈现三种相态——台阶附着（SA）相、脱附相和台面吸附（TA）相，三者由连续转变线分隔并在一个此前聚合物热力学中未曾预料的多临界点会聚；当 W ≲ 3 R_g^(2D) 时相图更简单，仅存在 SA 与 TA 相间的简单交叉，且无该多临界性；台阶上的扭折不改变这些行为。

Conclusion: 该研究填补了近平面台阶表面聚合物吸附的理论空白，揭示了由台面宽度调控的丰富相行为与多临界点；除理论意义外，还为在设计的台阶表面上通过"台阶附着-脱附"机制制备聚合物纳米线、调控聚合物构象提供了新途径。

Abstract: While polymer adsorption is theoretically well established on flat surfaces --- and somewhat understood on rough surfaces --- the common and practically relevant case of nearly flat stepped surfaces remains poorly studied. Here, we address this gap via extensive simulations of flexible polymer chains grafted at the step edge of vicinal surfaces. For wide terraces, the polymer-step interaction yields a stable step-attached (SA) phase, alongside the desorbed and terrace-adsorbed (TA) phases, all of them separated by continuous transition lines that meet at a multicritical point previously unanticipated in polymer thermodynamics. This scenario is observed for terrace widths $\mathcal{W} \gtrsim 3 R_g^{(2D)}$, being $R_g^{(2D)}$ the radius of gyration of TA polymers. Simpler phase diagrams, absent this multicriticality, are found for $\mathcal{W} \lesssim 3 R_g^{(2D)}$, where a simple crossover exists between the SA and TA phases. The presence of kinks at the steps does not alter these behaviors. Besides their theoretical relevance, these findings unveil an interesting route to produce polymer nanowires and control polymer conformation via step attachment-detachment on designed stepped surfaces.

</details>


### [8] [Dynamical theory of the East model. II. A path-probability formalism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23008)
*Chong Zha,Yuliang Jin*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

Abstract: Many systems are controlled by intermittent control variables that take on a random value for a random time interval. In the case of Poisson switching, these correspond to the Boolean cumulants of the jump distribution, and the kernel can be summed exactly. In the case of waiting times that follow a power-law distribution with a finite mean, it is highly probable that a long time interval will be covered by a single period, and we have shown that, as a result, the coefficients for long delays intersect in all orders, from Boolean cumulants to the usual moments of the jump law. The long-lag kernel thus becomes a superposition of frozen deterministic flux kernels, weighted by the expression $ξ^2p(ξ)$, and from this the corresponding main equation in closed form follows. For $2<μ<3$, where the noise correlation time is infinite, the second-order closure retains a finite error even in the presence of slow dynamics, whereas the main equation of the frozen flows in this limit yields an exact stationary mean value. The telegraphic process is the only case of noise with renewal steps for which second-order conclusions are exact. An exactly solvable model with additive and correlated multiplicative noise confirms these predictions.

Abstract: Under unitary dynamics preserving a global symmetry, bipartite entanglement typically grows while an initially broken symmetry is locally restored. Here, we show that this conventional scenario can be reversed when symmetry-breaking coherence is initially encoded in long-range entanglement. We investigate this phenomenon in a family of charge deformed crosscap states, characterized by antipodal entangled pairs with tunable entanglement and symmetry breaking. We first characterize their static properties, finding a Page-like entanglement profile and an entanglement asymmetry that vanishes for subsystems smaller than half the system size, but grows logarithmically for larger subsystems. For symmetric subsystems, we also analyze the symmetry-resolved entanglement and investigate how it evolves as the subsystem is continuously deformed away from a symmetric partition. Following a quench to a free-fermion tight-binding chain, we uncover an unconventional dynamics in which the entanglement entropy decreases whereas the entanglement asymmetry increases, so that initially symmetric subsystems dynamically develop local symmetry breaking. This behavior is quantitatively captured by a quasiparticle picture describing the propagation of antipodal correlations. Our results reveal how symmetry-preserving dynamics can redistribute globally encoded symmetry-breaking coherence, making it locally accessible, and establish the initial entanglement structure as a key ingredient in determining the fate of local symmetries out of equilibrium.

Abstract: Integrating Euclidean semiclassical gravity over real Riemannian metrics is subtle due to the conformal factor problem. A perturbative prescription was given by Gibbons-Hawking-Perry (GHP) where the integration contour for the conformal perturbations is rotated to be imaginary. A potential non-perturbative remedy is that the integration cycles associated to semiclassical saddle points should be deformed into the space of complex metrics in the sense of Picard-Lefschetz theory. Here we propose a non-perturbative construction of such complex integration cycles. This employs a 'forward-backward' gradient flow of the Einstein-Hilbert action, starting at a saddle point, along which the action has monotonically increasing real part and constant imaginary part. The flow is not parabolic. In a neighbourhood of a saddle point, the real part of the conformal factor flows forward and that of the conformal class backwards, with the opposite true of their imaginary parts. Starting the flow at a saddle point, then roughly speaking, one specifies the real part of the conformal class and the imaginary part of the conformal factor at the end of the flow. Solving the flow one finds the complex metrics along a one parameter path within the integration cycle of that saddle, and varying this final data generates the integration cycle, the Lefschetz thimble of the saddle point. We find that near to a real saddle point this reproduces the GHP prescription. We characterise the neighbourhood of a saddle where such forward-backward flows may exist, finding a weaker restriction than that of Kontsevich-Segal-Witten. We numerically demonstrate flows explicitly for 4-dimensional gravity, constructing sections through the integration contours for the saddles given by Euclidean de Sitter, and for Schwarzschild in a cavity.

</details>

Abstract: Standard Model fermion masses exhibit large hierarchies, spanning more than five orders of magnitude in the quark sector alone. In this paper, we study whether these hierarchies can be generated perturbatively at tree level in smooth heterotic Calabi-Yau compactifications, where the supergravity approximation remains under computational control. The former can be computed algebraically, while the latter require solving a coupled system of partial differential equations for the Ricci-flat metric, Hermitian Yang-Mills connections, and harmonic bundle-valued forms. Abstract: Heating need not destroy order. We show that it persists to arbitrarily high temperatures in simple quantum field theories in $2+1$ spacetime dimensions. In a $\mathbb Z_2\times\mathbb Z_2$ model of two real scalar fields, a sufficiently negative mixed quartic coupling drives one field to condense, spontaneously breaking its $\mathbb Z_2$ symmetry. When coupled to massless Dirac fermions through a Yukawa interaction, this condensate generates a fermion mass, thereby spontaneously breaking parity and time-reversal symmetries. The transitions are singular: the ground state expectation value of $\text{Tr}(A_iA_i)$ remains finite for $g< g_\ast^{BJ}$, whereas it diverges as $g$ approaches $g_\ast^{BJ}$ from above. We show that the two limiting ground states on either side of $g_\ast^{BJ}$ belong to distinct superselection sectors. Furthermore, as $g$ approaches $g_\ast^{BJ}$ from above, the ground state localizes on a submanifold (of codimension 2) of the gauge configuration space, with the spin and color of its glue components becoming locked.

Abstract: We study the classical $\frac{1}{16}$-BPS cohomology of $\mathcal{N}=4$ super-Yang-Mills theory with gauge group $\mathrm{SO}(2N)$ in the BMN sector, including the operators built with one $ε$-tensor. These operators are odd under the reflection in $\mathrm{O}(2N)$, carry the $\mathbb{Z}_2$ charge of a D3-brane wrapped on $\mathbb{RP}^3\subset\mathbb{RP}^5$, and form the Pfaffian sector. For $\mathrm{SO}(8)$ we determine this sector through level $j=18$, where the level is twice the total charge of an operator. All but one of its $106$ $\mathrm{SU}(3)_R$ multiplets are products of trace gravitons and excited Pfaffians. The exception is a singlet, represented by the singlet projection of the rank-four member of a four-term family of $ε$-tensor operators. We construct a covering space for the Pfaffian sector in which this family is closed at every rank, and the family defines a non-graviton class for every $N\ge 4$. Triality pairs the singlet with a reflection-even class that is fortuitous with respect to the trace covering. The two classes restrict, along the spinor and the vector embedding of $\mathfrak{so}(7)$ respectively, to the known non-graviton singlet of $\mathrm{SO}(7)$, which is lifted at first order. Their protection is compatible with the lifting in $\mathrm{SO}(7)$ because restriction to a subalgebra does not commute with the quantum corrections to the supercharge.

Training-data preparation is illustrated by 21 private, source-linked examples. For agents, it exposes 19 calculation tools with specified inputs and finite domains, connecting tool use to textbook derivations. The textbook collection and accompanying resources are available at https://github.com/xingyang-yu/StringQFT.

Abstract: The normal state of cuprate superconductors is believed to host precursor electron pairing above the superconducting transition temperature. These pre-formed pairs, which extend over an unknown temperature range, are expected to lack long-range phase coherence. Because spectroscopic evidence for this hypothesis is only indirect, new methods to probe these physics are needed. Nonlinear terahertz spectroscopy allows one to probe high-order time correlation functions and is therefore sensitive to fluctuations. Measurements of third-harmonic generation along the superconducting planes have revealed responses persisting above Tc, but a clear relation between these optical responses and specific properties of the fluctuating state has not yet been established. Here, we apply angle-resolved multidimensional terahertz spectroscopy to probe the optical nonlinearity of the Josephson tunneling resonance in La1.83Sr0.17CuO4 across the superconducting to normal state transition. We find that among all third-order nonlinear responses, the non-rephasing multidimensional signal is especially sensitive to non-mean field fluctuations of the order parameter. We measure a crossover occurring at T ~ Tc between a mean-field optical nonlinearity that dominates at low temperatures and one that is mediated by short-range superconducting fluctuations. We present a theoretical framework that connects the out-of-plane optical response in both regimes to the fluctuating in-plane superconducting state, and contrast the sensitivity of different types of terahertz nonlinearities to fluctuating order.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kiessling、Altshuler 和 Yuzbashyan 猜想：对于库仑赝势为零的各向同性 Eliashberg 自旋链泛函，正常态是临界温度及其以上的唯一极小化子，临界温度以下超导极小化子相差规范唯一，且相变连续。本文旨在严格确立该图景的非线性部分，从而完整证明该热力学猜想。

Result: 成功确立 Eliashberg 自旋链泛函热力学相变图景的非线性部分：正常态在临界温度及之上为唯一极小化子，其下超导极小化子相差规范唯一，相变连续。凝聚能在第一象限严格凸；对正的幂律衰减非增相互作用，极小化子存在且唯一（相差规范）。正常态极小化能量当且仅当二次型非负（含边际稳定）。非线性 Eliashberg 方程正解的存在与唯一性等价于正常态的线性不稳定性。对温度依赖族，最小化密度在加权 ℓ¹ 范数下连续。据此完整证明了 γ-模型、爱因斯坦声子与一般声子谱的热力学猜想。

Conclusion: 作者严格证明了各向同性 Eliashberg 自旋链泛函（库仑赝势为零）在临界温度处发生连续相变、超导极小化子相差规范唯一的热力学图景，并通过隐凸性、紧性估计与连续性分析，结合线性稳定性温度的唯一性及声子谱的谱输入，完全确立了该猜想，其中部分代数与凸分析论证在 Lean 中完成机器验证。

Abstract: Kiessling, Altshuler and Yuzbashyan conjectured the thermodynamic transition picture for the isotropic Eliashberg spin-chain functional with vanishing Coulomb pseudopotential: the normal state is the unique minimizer above and at a critical temperature, the superconducting minimizer below it is unique up to gauge, and the transition is continuous. We establish the nonlinear part of this picture. In the variables $ρ_n=\sin^2θ_n$, the condensation energy restricted to the first quadrant is strictly convex, and folding any admissible chain into that quadrant cannot increase its energy. For every positive, non-increasing interaction with power-law decay, hidden convexity and a compactness estimate give existence and uniqueness of the minimizer up to gauge. The normal state minimizes the energy exactly when its quadratic form is non-negative, including marginal stability. Among admissible chains, a positive solution of the nonlinear Eliashberg equations exists exactly when the normal state is linearly unstable and is then unique. For temperature-dependent families with pointwise continuity and locally uniform decay bounds, the minimizing density is continuous in a weighted $\ell^1$ norm. Together with uniqueness of the linear-stability temperature, these results prove the thermodynamic conjectures for the $γ$-model, Einstein phonons and general phonon spectra; the phonon applications use separately established spectral input. Finite-dimensional algebraic and convex-analytic components are machine-checked in Lean. The proofs were obtained independently of concurrent work that reaches overlapping conclusions.

</details>

Abstract: Periodic nanopatterning offers a route to tailoring vortex motion and the electromagnetic response of superconducting films, providing a basis for superconducting metamaterials. Here, we report a high-resolution top-down nanopatterning process for ultrathin epitaxial NbN films on sapphire substrates. Electron-beam lithography using hydrogen silsesquioxane (HSQ) and ZEP 530A resists was optimized through proximity-effect correction and adhesion-layer integration. Dense superconducting nanowire grids with pitches down to 70 nm were fabricated, alongside millimeter-square areas patterned with 100-nm-pitch hole arrays with high pattern fidelity. A thin Si capping layer was found to significantly improve HSQ adhesion on epitaxial NbN. The nanopatterns were successfully transferred into 5-nm-thick NbN films by reactive ion etching (RIE). Structural and dimensional characterization was performed using scanning electron microscopy (SEM), transmission electron microscopy (TEM), and atomic force microscopy (AFM), enabling cross-validation of nanoscale feature dimensions and etch quality. Electrical transport measurements reveal periodic resistance minima and switching-current oscillations at magnetic fields set by the hole-array pitch, consistent with enhanced vortex pinning when the vortex configuration matches the patterned lattice. Cryogenic THz time-domain spectroscopy further reveals a reduced superfluid density in the nanopatterned region relative to pristine NbN, while both regions have a spectroscopic superconducting transition near 14 K. These results establish a robust fabrication platform for superconducting nanopatterns targeting vortex pinning, metamaterials, and quantum-device applications.

TL;DR: 本文通过变分蒙特卡洛优化的视觉Transformer神经量子态，研究三角与蜂窝谢尔宾斯基垫片上自旋-1/2 XY铁磁体的基态，发现横向关联函数呈代数衰减（η≈0.12–0.32），表明量子相位涨落并未破坏准长程序。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分形几何已被证明能增强超导序参量振幅，但超导相位在此类晶格上的命运远不明确。经典XY模型在有限分叉分形上无有限温度相变，因为涡旋能量随系统增长保持有界；但该结论针对无限垫片和热涡旋，而真实分形结构是有限的，且未回答T=0时相位与自身量子涨落竞争的情形。本文旨在厘清量子相位相干性在基态能否存续。

Conclusion: 在T=0基态下，量子相位涨落未破坏分形超导网络的相位相干性，准长程序得以存续。作者进一步讨论了该结果对有限垫片在低但非零温度下超导响应的启示，表明这类分形结构有望在低温下维持可观的超导相位相干。

Abstract: Fractal geometry has recently been shown to enhance the amplitude of the superconducting order parameter, but the fate of the superconducting phase on such lattices is far less clear. Classical XY models on finitely ramified fractals have no finite-temperature transition, as the energy of a vortex stays bounded as the system grows. However, that statement concerns an infinite gasket and thermal vortices, while real fractal structures are finite by construction. It also leaves open what happens at $T = 0$, where the phase competes not with temperature but with its own quantum fluctuations. In this work we address whether superconducting phase coherence survives at $T=0$. %Whether coherence survives those is the prior question, and the one we address here. To that end, we study the spin-$1/2$ XY ferromagnet, the phase sector of a Josephson network with at most one Cooper pair per island, thus capturing the maximum effect of quantum phase fluctuations, on both triangular and honeycomb Sierpiński gaskets. By using vision-transformer neural quantum states optimized by variational Monte Carlo, we reach generations up to $G = 5$, that is $366$ sites for the triangular gasket and $609$ for the honeycomb one, well beyond the reach of exact diagonalization. We find that the transverse two-point correlator decays algebraically, $C_{xy}(r) \sim r^{-η}$ with $η\simeq 0.12\!-\!0.32$, independent of distance measured along the graph, along the fractal boundary, or in the embedding plane. Quantum phase fluctuations thus leave quasi-long-range order intact in the ground state. We further discuss the implication of these results for the superconducting response of a finite gasket at low but nonzero temperature.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文证明，在吸引的约化BCS模型中，成对（paired）子空间可以在能量意义上从完整费米子希尔伯特空间中自发选出：零温度下固定平均粒子数或固定偶数粒子数的所有基态都位于成对子空间内，仅奇数粒子数时需留下一个未配对费米子；在此子空间内最小化广义-P表示的正超态可导出BCS及数投影BCS态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统BCS与数投影BCS变分处理都限制在“成对希尔伯特子空间”中（动量与自旋相反的态要么同时空、要么同时占），但这一子空间本身能否从完整费米子希尔伯特空间中由能量最低原则自发选出，此前并未严格论证。作者希望填补这一逻辑缺口。

Method: 利用守恒投影算符区分单占态与空态/双占态，将完整希尔伯特空间分解为不变子空间（sectors）；对吸引的约化BCS模型在各子空间间直接比较能量。随后在成对子空间内，用赝自旋相干态的正广义-P表示进行最小化，并分别在平均粒子数与固定粒子数约束下求解。

Result: 零温度下，固定平均粒子数时的每个基态都位于成对子空间；固定偶数粒子数时结论同样成立且保持总粒子数守恒；固定奇数粒子数时由宇称要求恰好留下一个未配对费米子。在成对子空间内，最小化选择出非零对振幅的共同相位并确定其大小，BCS态与数投影BCS态分别作为相应粒子数约束下的特定极小值点出现。

Conclusion: 成对子空间并非人为假设，而是可由能量最小化从完整希尔伯特空间中严格选出，从而为BCS及数投影BCS变分处理提供了坚实的微观依据；奇数粒子数情形由宇称自然解释。

Abstract: Conventional Bardeen--Cooper--Schrieffer (BCS) and number-projected BCS variational treatments are formulated within a paired Hilbert subspace, where modes with opposite momenta and spins are either both empty or both occupied. Here we ask whether this paired subspace can itself be selected energetically from the full fermionic Hilbert space of the attractive reduced BCS model. Using conserved projectors that distinguish singly occupied states from empty and doubly occupied ones, we decompose the full Hilbert space into invariant sectors. A direct energy comparison between sectors shows that, at zero temperature, every ground state at fixed average particle number lies in the paired subspace. The same holds at fixed even particle number, with the comparison preserving the total particle number, while at fixed odd particle number one fermion remains unpaired, as required by parity. Within the resulting paired subspace, minimization over positive generalized-$P$ representations of pseudospin coherent states selects a common phase for the nonzero pair amplitudes and determines their magnitudes. BCS and number-projected BCS states arise as particular minimizers under the respective particle-number constraints.

</details>


### [50] [Ab initio Engineering of Topological Superconductivity in Substrate-Supported Magnetic Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23016)
*András Lászlóffy,Balazs Ujfalussy*

TL;DR: 本文结合多种第一性原理方法，系统扫描了生长在 Nb(110) 表面不同覆盖层上、多种磁性原子链在不同几何构型下的磁性与超导性质，旨在为拓扑 Majorana 边缘态的设计提供可行路径。


<details>

In the next step, we solve the Bogoliubov-de Gennes equations using a band-theoretical method. Depending on the magnetic structure and atomic configuration, the superconducting spectra may exhibit topologically trivial minigaps, broad overlapping Shiba bands, or localized zero-energy edge states accompanied by sizable topological minigaps. In particular, Fe chains on Ag/Nb(110) are found to display pronounced signatures associated with topological superconductivity, including strongly localized zero-energy edge states and indications of Shiba band inversion. We further demonstrate that changes in the spin structure or a small amount of spatial relaxation can substantially modify the superconducting band structure and the localization properties of the edge states.

Abstract: A persistent mystery of CuO_2-based high temperature superconductivity has been the mechanism and consequences of time reversal ($\mathcal{T}$)-symmetry breaking. Although theoretical hypotheses focus upon persistent electronic currents as a potential cause, these have been invisible to direct experimental probes. Here, by imaging the energy-resolved density of electronic states $N(r,E)$ in $\mathrm{Bi_2Sr_2CaCu_2O_8}$, we search for the specific signatures of flowing supercurrents in a disordered $d$-wave superconductor. Then, for hole-densities 0.17 $\leq p \leq$ 0.22 we observe statistically repeatable networks of supercurrent flows. Crucially, these supercurrents satisfy Kirchhoff's law at junctions within each network. Further, theoretical predictions for supercurrent speeds $V$ in disordered $d$-wave superconductors are consistent with measured 1250 m/s $< V <$ 4000 m/s, and with reported magnetic field amplitudes $|B| \approx$ 0.1 mT Thus, detection and visualization of these supercurrent loop networks provides unique perspectives on $\mathcal{T}$-symmetry breaking in cuprates. It also emphasizes the accelerating utility of ANN techniques for physics discovery from high data volume quantum matter research.

Abstract: Unconventional superconductivity in rhombohedral $n$-layer graphene exhibits a rich phase diagram with multiple distinct superconducting regions, whose microscopic origins remain unclear. Here, we investigate the competition between charge-density-wave (CDW) and pair-density-wave (PDW) instabilities near the inner van Hove singularities (VHSs), using a patch renormalization-group approach with the realistic band structures incorporated. We identify two key findings. First, we find an effective attractive interaction emerging from purely repulsive interactions through RG flow in the presence of $C_3$ symmetry breaking. The resulting interaction structure, with two repulsive couplings and one attractive coupling, gives rise to competing CDW and PDW instabilities. Second, the competition is controlled by the detuning from the VHS. While CDW dominates near the VHS filling and at higher temperatures, PDW becomes the leading instability on either side of the VHS and at lower temperatures. This mechanism naturally produces two superconducting regions separated by a CDW-dominated regime, providing a possible qualitative explanation for the experimentally observed separation between SC1 and SC2.

</details>

Abstract: Antiferromagnetic skyrmions are promising topologically protected entities for robust storage and THz transport of information. Here, we demonstrate the imprinting of antiferromagnetic skyrmions within a few picoseconds in a coupled ferromagnetic/antiferromagnetic bilayer with a thermal laser pulse. We identify a threshold energy required to initiate the fused skyrmion phase and show that this can be modulated by a magnetic field, enabling deterministic nucleation of antiferromagnetic skyrmions. Compared to conventional slow field-cooling processes to control antiferromagnets we show that laser-induced skyrmion imprinting can be orders of magnitude faster and provides an experimentally feasible route towards ultrafast creation and manipulation of antiferromagnetic skyrmions.

Abstract: The inverse Faraday effect converts the angular momentum of circularly polarized light into a quasistatic magnetization, providing a fundamental route to ultrafast optical control of magnetic fields. We develop an analytical quantum theory of this effect in intrinsic graphene, where massless Dirac fermions, electron-hole symmetry, and weak spin-orbit coupling suppress conventional population-based interpretations. We show that in intrinsic graphene, the helicity-dependent orbital magnetization arises from the off-diagonal zero-harmonic component of the second-order density matrix, rather than from intraband carrier circulation or diagonal population imbalance. We obtain closed expressions for the induced temporal surface magnetization, its finite-pulse dynamics, and the nonlocal stray magnetic field generated by the two-dimensional (2D) source. Optical helicity, frequency, pulse envelope, and beam profile independently control the response. Our results identify intrinsic graphene as an ideal platform for coherent orbital optomagnetism in 2D Dirac matter.

Abstract: Radiative heat transfer involving complex nano-objects is difficult to evaluate due to their sub-wavelength size, which induces deviation to macroscopic Planck's opaque surface-to-surface formalism, and due to the small distances involved, which lead to near-field radiative (photon tunneling) effects. As a result, thermal radiation has mostly been neglected in analyses of thermal transport in nanowires. Here, we report on calculations of thermal emission by single nanowires and on the radiative interaction between suspended silicon nanowires and flat substrates. We observe that metallic nanowires can be particularly emissive in comparison to wires made of other materials. While thermal emission from an isolated silicon nanowire is lower than Stefan-Boltzmann's prediction, the radiative exchange with a substrate can be larger by one order of magnitude. The considered configurations also allow benchmarking various levels of common or original approximations, including, in near field, the proximity approximation, the thickness-dependent PA, the electrostatic approach, and the effective emissivity in the far field.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 采用对称性拓扑场论（SymTFT）方法，将对称性编码到高一维的拓扑理论中；以（1+1）维融合范畴对称性为核心实例，阐述 SymTFT 如何刻画反常、分类能隙相、组织无能隙相的对称结构，并对高维对称性、连续对称性、费米子系统、混合态及时空对称性等推广方向作综述性梳理。

Result: 在（1+1）维融合范畴对称性中，SymTFT 成功刻画反常、对能隙相进行分类并组织无能隙相的对称结构；同时展示了该框架向高维、连续、费米子、混合态和时空对称性等多种情形的扩展进展。

Conclusion: SymTFT 为量子多体系统对称性提供了一个统一而强大的框架，能有效分离对称性数据与系统细节，并在相分类与对称结构分析中展现出广泛适用性和拓展潜力。

Abstract: The symmetry topological field theory (SymTFT) provides a unified framework for ordinary and generalized symmetries of quantum many-body systems. By encoding symmetry in a topological theory in one higher dimension, it separates symmetry data from the details of a specific quantum system. In this review, we give a pedagogical introduction to this framework and survey its applications to phases of quantum matter. For fusion category symmetries in ${(1+1)}$d, we explain how the SymTFT characterizes anomalies, classifies gapped phases, and organizes the symmetry structure of gapless phases. We also give a broad overview of further developments of this framework, including extensions to symmetries in higher dimensions, continuous symmetries, fermionic systems, mixed states, and spacetime symmetries.

</details>

*Subir Sachdev*

<details>

In the deep triangular potential limit, the electrons form the topologically trivial "Wigner molecular crystal'' of three-electron artificial atoms, a state recently imaged at zero magnetic field in moiré bilayers. As the potential depth is lowered, molecular charge order remains while quantized Hall response emerges. At the honeycomb point, where each cell contains three electrons and two degenerate moiré minima, wells are either singly or doubly occupied and the doublons provide an emergent Ising degree of freedom. Starting from modest interaction strength, we find short-range doublon correlations within the Chern insulator which grow with interaction strength and eventually break sublattice symmetry, forming a Néel CDW with long-range order between doublons. The topological molecular regime lies at twist angles and magnetic fields directly accessible to current experiments. The methodological advances presented provide a scalable framework for exploring a broad range of questions in correlated quantum matter in the presence of magnetic fields.

Abstract: Phase-incoherent preformed excitons have long been anticipated in excitonic insulators, yet their characteristic signatures and underlying physics remain elusive, as the electrical neutrality of excitons hinders direct probes of condensate phase coherence. Theoretically, we attribute this intriguing separation to two distinct processes governing the loss of excitonic order by its own emergent excitations: boson-driven phase decoherence and fermion-driven pair breaking. The self-consistent microscopic calculation quantitatively captures our key observations, establishing an excitonic realization of preformed-pairing physics.

Abstract: We investigate the classification of continuous families of translation-invariant injective matrix product states on an infinite chain, parametrized by a paracompact Hausdorff space $X$ and subject to a compact Hausdorff symmetry $G$. Several examples are given over various parameter spaces where this invariant is nontrivial.

D 114, 064021 (2026)], the even-dimensional proof requires solving a nonlinear connection problem. In $d=10$, where one explicit profile degenerates to the zero solution, we give a formal argument for the existence of a second nontrivial smooth profile in addition to the single nontrivial explicit one.

Abstract: We establish an upper bound on the critical temperature shift of three-dimensional Bose gases with general nonnegative finite-range interactions. In the dilute regime $aρ^{1/3}\to0$, we prove $T_c(ρ)/T_c^{(0)}(ρ) \le 1 + (C_{\rm H}+o(1))\sqrt{aρ^{1/3}}$, where \[
  C_{\rm H}=\frac{2^{7/2}\sqrtπ}{3ζ(3/2)^{2/3}}
  =3.523992\ldots \] is the coefficient predicted by Huang in 1999. This improves the earlier rigorous upper bound of Seiringer and Ueltschi in 2009. More precisely, we prove the exponential decay of the correlations above the stated temperature through the Feynman--Kac representation, for both integrable and hard-core interactions.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 1) 对v=u^3，四孤子条件恰在重数一时成立。2) 对图v=q(u)（deg q≥3），P存在当且仅当q为奇三次或奇五次，故猜想想对所有多项式图成立。3) Hietarinta列表三个族中，每个不可约成员（差常数因子）是其色散簇上唯一的此类多项式，且三个都满足任意N的N孤子条件：两个属于BKP层级，Hietarinta四次方程经证明也满足。4) 对平移双曲线，排除了过原点情形及所有次数≤8的P。5) 图上的重数一解是已知双线性方程，新结论为不存在其他解。

Abstract: The componentwise three-soliton conjecture of Part~I states that if an even polynomial $P$ with $P(0)=0$ satisfies Hirota's three-soliton condition on a non-hyperplane component of multiplicity one of its dispersion variety, then it satisfies the $N$-soliton condition there for every $N$. Restrictions: if the top homogeneous part of the equation of the component has a nonlinear irreducible factor, then $P$ is a multiple of a quadratic form; the order of $P$ at the origin is tied to the multiplicities of the component and of the doubled component; a component through the origin has odd multiplicity; the condition passes to all limits of the rescaled component, and the Newton polygon of a plane component has edges of only ten directions. Classifications: on the cubic graph $v=u^3$, where the four-soliton condition holds exactly in multiplicity one; on the graphs $v=q(u)$ with $°q\ge3$, where $P$ exists only if $q$ is an odd cubic or an odd quintic, so the conjecture holds for all graphs of polynomials; and for three families of Hietarinta's list, where each irreducible member is, up to a constant factor, the only such polynomial on its dispersion variety. All three satisfy the $N$-soliton condition for every $N$, so the conjecture holds for them: two belong to the BKP hierarchy, and for Hietarinta's quartic equation, known to satisfy it for $N\le4$, we prove it by fusing resonant momenta and counting zeros on its curve. For translated hyperbolas we exclude the one through the origin and all $P$ of degree at most eight. The solutions of multiplicity one on graphs are known bilinear equations; new is that there are no others. The conjecture remains open.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Let $P=A_N\cdots A_1$ be a product of independent standard real or complex Gaussian matrices of arbitrary fixed sizes. For complex entries we determine the exact leading terms, of the form $κ\,\varepsilon^C(\log1/\varepsilon)^q$, of the operator-norm and Frobenius small-ball probabilities of $P$, together with the corresponding asymptotics of the Laplace transform of $\|P^2\|_{F}$. The exponent $C$ is the known diversity order, while the logarithmic power follows a filling rule: when fermions are placed on ladders of states determined by the sizes and the last occupied level has degeneracy $μ$ and contains $h$ particles, $q=h(μ-h)$. The determinant of this last shell expands in Plücker coordinates graded by the Schubert cells of the Grassmannian $\mathrm{Gr}(h,μ)$, with leading coefficients proportional to normalized degrees of Schubert varieties. For real entries we prove that the Frobenius small-ball probability has an exact positive leading term of order $\varepsilon^{C/2}(\log1/\varepsilon)^q$ and that the operator-norm probability has the same order, which gives a proof of the known real log canonical threshold and its multiplicity that needs no explicit resolution of singularities. We evaluate the real leading coefficient in closed form for every shell with at most one logarithm, a class that contains all products of two factors, and we conjecture its general form, which agrees with the classical constant of vector chains. Applications include the outage probability of cascaded multi-antenna channels at high signal-to-noise ratio and the Bayesian evidence of deep linear networks.

Abstract: We develop an extended Hamiltonian formalism for action-dependent (Herglotz-type) classical field theories within the framework of multicontact geometry. While the standard Hamiltonian description of these theories is formulated on the restricted multimomentum bundle, we work instead on the extended multimomentum bundle, which carries an additional energy-type coordinate together with a canonical multicontact structure. In this setting, an extended Hamiltonian multicontact system is governed by a Hamiltonian density and its associated Hamiltonian one-form, and the submanifold they determine recovers the restricted formalism. We characterise the admissible one-forms and prove that the associated Hamilton-de Donder-Weyl field equations, stated equivalently for sections, multivector fields, and Ehresmann connections, are mutually equivalent. We then establish the precise relation between the extended and restricted formalisms in both the regular (multicontact) and the almost-regular (premulticontact) settings, the latter requiring a constraint algorithm. The formalism is illustrated with two examples: the action-dependent Klein-Gordon equation, which includes the telegrapher equation as a particular case, and the heat equation, whose contactification yields Burgers' equation.

Abstract: A rheometer is commonly used to measure the input-output relation for analyzing the relaxation of stress for rheological materials. Here, the input is a time-harmonic torque and the output is the angular displacement. This paper aims to propose an exact inversion scheme of the relaxation function from two input-output relations only having different moments of inertia. The relaxation function is represented by a Prony's series of known number of terms, with a positive constant term and terms with distinct relaxation times. The augmented system relates the material parameters to its characteristic roots. These roots are the poles of the transfer function. Here the transfer function is the Laplace transform of the output divided by that of the input, and it is a rational function in the Laplace variable. The poles of the transfer function are recovered using Pade's approximation. By measuring two input-output relations only having different moments of inertia, we can rigorously recover the relaxation function. Thus, we note a clear link between the characteristic roots and the poles. Numerical experiments reconstruct the relaxation function from synthetic angular displacements corresponding to two given moments of inertia based on our inversion method. The results show the effect of measurement noise on the recovered characteristic roots and relaxation function.

Abstract: We show that, given a sufficiently regular time-dependent density, there exists a unique potential such that the Schrödinger equation with this external potential has the prescribed one-particle density. Our method of proof is to rewrite the problem as a nonlinear Schrödinger equation with a loss of derivatives, which we in turn handle with a completion-of-the-square argument. Result: 证明了无穷小协变 Stone-von Neumann 定理：对实初等 C*-动力系统，每个无穷小 Heisenberg 表示都是无穷小 Schrödinger 表示的若干副本的直和；同时得到实 C*-动力系统位置算子和动量算子的显式描述，并构造出二者的解析向量之自然公共不变核，所有定义域与可积性问题均已完整处理。

Conclusion: 本文成功将协变 Stone-von Neumann 定理推广到无穷小形式，通过“Heisenberg 可微-可积”双向对应，把酉表示分类转化为正则自伴稠定算子层面的分类，从而在 Hilbert C*-模与实初等 C*-动力系统的框架下同时统一了全局酉表述与无穷小算子表述，并为位置/动量算子提供了显式构造与解析向量核，确保技术严密性。

Abstract: In a previous paper, the authors established the Covariant Stone-von Neumann Theorem, which generalizes the Stone-von Neumann Theorem in quantum mechanics and yields a classification of all unitary Heisenberg representations of an abelian elementary $ C^{\ast} $-dynamical system up to unitary equivalence. In this sequel to that paper, we establish an infinitesimal version of the Covariant Stone-von Neumann Theorem in the setting of real elementary $ C^{\ast} $-dynamical systems. Firstly, we generalize Stone's Theorem to secure a one-to-one correspondence between the strongly continuous one-parameter unitary groups and the regular self-adjoint densely defined operators on a Hilbert $ C^{\ast} $-module. Secondly, we define the infinitesimal Heisenberg representations and the infinitesimal Schrödinger representation of a real $ C^{\ast} $-dynamical system. Thirdly, we construct a one-to-one correspondence between the unitary Heisenberg representation and the infinitesimal Heisenberg representations of a real $ C^{\ast} $-dynamical system, providing a rigorous two-way passage--which we call ``Heisenberg differentiability-integrability''--between the global unitary formulation of Heisenberg covariance and its infinitesimal formulation in terms of regular self-adjoint densely defined operators. This allows us to prove the Infinitesimal Covariant Stone-von Neumann Theorem: For a real elementary $ C^{\ast} $-dynamical system, every infinitesimal Heisenberg representation is a direct sum of copies of the infinitesimal Schrödinger representation. Lastly, we obtain explicit descriptions of the position and momentum operators for a real $ C^{\ast} $-dynamical system and construct a natural common invariant core of analytic vectors for them. All requisite technical domain and integrability issues are fully accounted for.

</details>


### [80] [Zero mode surface of Hermitian two-by-two matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23087)
*Andreas Sykora*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文对厄米2×2矩阵（Dirac算子）的零模曲面（亦称Clifford谱）进行了完整分类。利用欧几里得群下的协变性、酉共轭不变性及奇异值分解，将任意m元厄米2×2矩阵组化为标准形，并证明其Clifford谱的拓扑仅由三角不等式判定，且永不为空。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Clifford谱（零模曲面）是描述Dirac算子谱性质的重要对象，但其拓扑结构此前缺乏统一、系统的分类。作者旨在通过将任意厄米2×2矩阵组化为标准形，给出零模曲面的完全拓扑分类，从而厘清该对象在不同参数下的几何形态。

Method: 利用Dirac算子在欧几里得群作用下的协变性及酉共轭不变性，结合奇异值分解（SVD），将任意m元厄米2×2矩阵组（不足3个时补零矩阵）化为标准形(b₁σ₁, b₂σ₂, b₃σ₃, 0, …, 0)，其中b₁≥b₂≥b₃≥0，σᵢ为Pauli矩阵。由于补零矩阵仅将Clifford谱嵌入更高维空间，故只需研究三个矩阵的情形。在该标准形下显式给出Dirac算子的行列式闭式表达，并求出其临界点。

Result: 零模曲面的拓扑结构完全由(b₁,b₂,b₃)的三角不等式决定：当b₃=0时，零模曲面由一个或两个点组成；当b₁>b₂+b₃时，为两个不相交的拓扑球面；当b₁=b₂+b₃时，为两个在一点处相切的拓扑球面；当b₁<b₂+b₃时，为单个拓扑球面。该分类可通过海伦公式加以解释。

Conclusion: 任意厄米2×2矩阵组的Clifford谱（零模曲面）永不为空，其拓扑完全由(b₁,b₂,b₃)是否满足三角不等式所支配，并据此得到完整分类。

Abstract: We classify the zero mode surfaces, also called Clifford spectra, of Hermitian $2\times 2$ matrices. Using the covariance of the Dirac operator under the Euclidean group and its invariance under unitary conjugation, together with a singular value decomposition, every $m$-tuple of Hermitian $2\times 2$ matrices, after adjoining zero matrices if $m<3$, is reduced to the normal form $(b_1σ_1,b_2σ_2,b_3σ_3,0,\dots,0)$ with $b_1\geq b_2\geq b_3\geq 0$. Since adding a vanishing matrix only embeds the Clifford spectrum into a higher-dimensional space, it suffices to consider three matrices. In this normal form the determinant of the Dirac operator is given in closed form and its critical points are determined explicitly. The zero mode surface consists of one or two points if $b_3=0$. Otherwise it consists of two disjoint topological spheres if $b_1>b_2+b_3$, of two topological spheres touching at one point if $b_1=b_2+b_3$, and of a single topological sphere if $b_1<b_2+b_3$. The topology is thus governed by the triangle inequality for $(b_1,b_2,b_3)$, which can be interpreted with Heron's formula. In particular, the Clifford spectrum of any tuple of Hermitian $2\times 2$ matrices is never empty.

</details>

### [81] [Dynamics and transport in LiF using classical and machine learned interatomic potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21900)
*Harvey L. Devereux,Margaret-Ann Withington,Alin M. Elena,Cillian Cockrell,Kostya Trachenko*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过分子动力学模拟熔融氟化锂，比较经典势（Buckingham、Born-Huggins-Mayer）与机器学习原子间势（MACE-MP-0b3 基础模型及其微调版本），发现机器学习势能准确复现实验剪切粘度，并探究了其微观机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前经典势计算的熔融氟化锂粘度被系统性低估，且这一偏差被归因于熔化温度被压低。作者希望检验机器学习原子间势能否同时改善粘度和熔化温度的预测，并从微观结构与动力学层面揭示粘度差异的来源。

Method: 对熔融氟化锂进行分子动力学模拟，对比四种势函数：经典 Buckingham 势、Born-Huggins-Mayer 势、以及 MACE-MP-0b3（small）基础模型和其微调版本。随后比较各势的剪切粘度与熔化温度，并结合单粒子与集体电荷输运动力学、长波极限下的集体弹性运动等微观量进行分析。

Result: 开箱即用的 MACE-MP-0b3 尽管本身非极化，却能与极化势相当地准确复现实验剪切粘度；而经典势低估粘度、且熔化温度被显著压低。机器学习势能改善熔化温度的低估，但基础模型与微调版本之间差异明显。微观层面，经典势因固定电荷而产生更硬、更高频的单粒子及集体电荷输运动力学；机器学习势则在长波极限下表现出更强弹性的集体运动，主导动量扩散并由此提升剪切粘度。

Conclusion: 机器学习原子间势（尤其是 MACE-MP-0b3）能在无需极化的情况下准确预测熔融氟化锂的粘度和改善熔化温度。粘度增强源于长波极限下更强的集体弹性运动对动量扩散的主导作用，而非固定电荷体系的僵硬高频动力学。

Abstract: We perform molecular dynamics simulations of molten Lithium Fluoride, comparing the classical Buckingham and Born-Huggins-Mayer potentials with machine-learnt interatomic potentials - the MACE-MP-0b3 (small) foundation model potential, and a fine-tuned version thereof. We find that the MACE-MP-0b3, out-of-the-box, is able to accurately reproduce the experimental shear viscosity across the liquid state comparable with polarisable potentials despite not itself being polarisable. Previous viscosities computed using classical potentials are under-predicted, which has previously been attributed to a suppressed melting temperature. Prompted by this we compare the melting points of these potentials. We find that the machine-learnt potentials address the significant underestimation of the melting point of the classical potentials, though the refined potential and the foundation model differ significantly between themselves. We probe the microscopic structure and dynamics of each model to further elucidate the considerable change in viscosity between classical and machine-learnt potentials. We find that, despite their lower melting temperature, classical potentials produce considerably stiffer, higher frequency, single-particle and collective charge-transport dynamics on account of their fixed charges. However, in the long-wavelength limit, the many-body machine-learnt potentials feature more elastic collective motion that governs the diffusion of momentum and is therefore responsible for the enhanced shear viscosity.

</details>


### [82] [quantEM: Quantitative Electron Microscopy Analysis from Differentiable Physics to Neural Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21903)
*Colin Ophus,Arthur RC McCray,Georgios Varnavides,Stephanie M Ribet*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: quantEM 是一个基于 PyTorch 与 Zarr 的开源 Python 软件包，通过统一的微分正向模型框架，将定量电子显微学的多种模态分析（取向/应变映射、叠层成像、层析成像、X射线与EELS能谱）整合在同一平台，支持 CPU/GPU、可保存续算，并能组装如叠层层析等组合方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 定量电子显微学的分析越来越依赖求解带物理正向模型的逆问题。现有开源软件虽已普及，但大多围绕单一模态组织，导致多模态分析需要在不同软件包间迁移数据，且新方法难以复用已有物理模型与优化器。

Method: quantEM 的做法是：为每种分析方法编写可微分的正向模型，并从组件库中选择表示方法、损失函数、约束条件和优化器进行组装。计算采用 PyTorch 张量与自动微分，存储采用 Zarr，从而实现统一的数据结构、可视化工具、CPU/GPU 运行以及断点保存与恢复。

Result: 已实现多个模块：取向与应变映射、直接与迭代式叠层成像（ptychography）、基于神经表示的层析成像、X射线能谱与电子能量损失谱（EELS）；并展示了如何从现有组件组装出如叠层成像层析（ptychographic tomography）这样的组合方法。

Conclusion: quantEM 以 MIT 许可证发布，附带文档、教程和精选示例数据集，为多模态定量电子显微学分析提供了一个统一、可扩展、可复用的开源平台。

Abstract: Quantitative electron microscopy includes imaging, diffraction, spectroscopy, and tomography measurements, and the analysis of these measurements increasingly requires solving inverse problems with physical forward models. Open-source software has made many of these analyses widely available, but most packages are organized around a single modality, so that multimodal analyses require moving data between packages, and new methods cannot easily reuse the physical models and optimizers of existing ones. Here we present quantEM, an open-source Python package for quantitative electron microscopy that uses PyTorch tensors for computation and automatic differentiation, and Zarr for storage. We implement each analysis method by writing a differentiable forward model and choosing a representation, loss function, constraints, and optimizer from a library of components, so that every method runs on CPUs and GPUs, can be saved and resumed, and uses the same calibrated data structures and visualization tools. We describe the current modules, including orientation and strain mapping, direct and iterative ptychography, tomography with neural representations, and X-ray and electron energy-loss spectroscopy, and show how we assemble combined methods such as ptychographic tomography from existing components. quantEM is available under the MIT license, together with documentation, tutorials, and curated example datasets.

</details>


### [83] [Weight-Space Programming for Materials Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22013)
*Sk Md Ahnaf Akif Alvi,Raymundo Arróyave*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出用语言模型直接“书写”小型神经网络的权重（权重空间编程），为零标签/少标签的材料发现提供初始排序模型，在合金、晶体及相平衡目标上显著超越了简单描述符和子体系外推，并作为高斯过程与贝叶斯神经网络先验提升了少标签排序与贝叶斯优化效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 材料发现活动初期几乎没有任何目标性质的测量数据，用于筛选候选或生成器输出的排序模型必须仅凭极少数标签构建，急需一种无需训练标签、能提供可靠起始点的通用先验方法。

Method: 借鉴权重空间编程（语言模型以文本直接生成小网络的权重），让通用大语言模型在给定任务描述、输入名称及一个16隐藏单元、全部权重初始为零的网络后，直接填出全部权重，从而得到零标签排序网络；并将该网络作为高斯过程的先验均值、贝叶斯神经网络的均值及晶体生成器的筛选器。

Result: 零标签下最强模型对实验合金排序 Spearman 系数达 0.64–0.79（五种最佳简单描述符为 0.47–0.62）；对晶体体模量排序达 0.95（逆原子体积为 0.89）；对 Thermo-Calc 计算的 35 个相平衡目标平均 0.67（接近二元/三元计算外推的 0.70）。作为高斯过程先验均值，合金排序从五标签的 0.35 提升至 0.69，晶体从 0.15 提升至 0.95；在 5 个实验任务中的 4 个及 33 个计算目标中的 13–16 个显著降低了贝叶斯优化遗憾；作为贝叶斯神经网络均值与生成器筛选器时，达到与用 400 个标注结构训练的标准模型相当的水平。增益集中在最初几十个标签，且仅对规则或子体系外推无法排序的目标生效。

Conclusion: 大语言模型通过权重空间编程可为零标签材料发现提供有效先验，显著提升少标签排序、贝叶斯优化与生成器筛选性能，其优势主要体现在数据极度稀缺的起始阶段和现有启发式无法覆盖的目标上。

Abstract: A materials discovery campaign often begins with almost no measurements of the property it is searching for, and the models that rank candidates or screen the output of a generator then have to be built from those first few labels. Following recent work on weight-space programming, in which a language model writes the weights of a small network directly as text, we show that a general-purpose model, given the task in words, the names of the inputs and a network of 16 hidden units with every weight set to zero, can provide this starting point by filling in all of the weights from what it has learned about materials. With no labels, the networks of the strongest model rank experimental alloys at Spearman co-efficient = 0.64 to 0.79, where the best of five simple descriptors reaches 0.47 to 0.62. They rank the bulk modulus of crystals added during generative discovery loops at 0.95, where the inverse volume per atom reaches 0.89, and 35 phase-equilibrium targets computed with Thermo-Calc at a mean of 0.67, close to the 0.70 of an extrapolation from calculated binary and ternary data. As the prior mean of a Gaussian process they raise the ranking at five labels from 0.35 to 0.69 on alloys and from 0.15 to 0.95 on crystals, and they lower the regret of Bayesian optimization significantly on 4 of 5 experimental tasks and on 13 to 16 of 33 computed targets, depending on how multiple tests are corrected, without raising it significantly on any. The same networks also serve as the mean of a Bayesian neural network and as a screen for the samples of two crystal generators, where they do as well as standard models trained on 400 labeled structures. The gain is confined to the first tens of labels and to targets that no rule or sub-system extrapolation already ranks.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出一种在势能约束密度泛函理论（cDFT）框架内实现的广义微动弹性带（NEB）方法，可同时优化原子位置、晶格和磁自由度，用于计算磁结构体系的过渡路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 NEB 计算通常局限于固定晶胞内的原子位移，无法同时处理原子、晶格和磁自由度耦合的过渡过程；本文旨在填补这一空白，实现三类自由度的一致耦合优化。

Method: 在 cDFT 框架下实现广义 NEB，一致耦合原子力、应力和自旋力矩；支持固定晶胞与变晶胞两种方式，并适用于共线与非共线磁构型，在 NEB 优化中纳入磁力矩和广义晶胞坐标。

Conclusion: 该方法能有效利用力、应力和自旋力矩，在变晶胞条件下同时优化原子、晶格与磁自由度，为磁结构相关相变与自旋翻转等复杂过渡过程提供了可靠的计算工具。

Abstract: We present a generalized nudged elastic band (NEB) implementation within potential-based constrained density-functional theory (cDFT) for calculating transition pathways that simultaneously optimize atomic, lattice, and magnetic degrees of freedom. Unlike standard fixed-cell NEB calculations restricted to atomic displacements, our framework consistently couples atomic forces, stresses, and spin torques. The method supports fixed- and variable-cell approaches for collinear and non- collinear magnetic configurations, by incorporating magnetic torques and generalized cell coordinates within the NEB optimization. To validate the methodology, we present benchmark calculations for representative magnetostructural systems. Specifically, we investigate the bcc-to-hcp transformation in Fe and the antiferromagnetic-to-ferromagnetic transition in FeRh using the variable-cell formalism. Furthermore, we examine spin-reversal processes in two-dimensional CrI3 to analyze the influence of spin-orbit coupling on transition paths and their associated energy barriers.

</details>

*Robert Lipton,Prakash K. C.*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于预测脆性材料损伤与断裂的场理论，结合非局部变形梯度、本构定律驱动的相场、牛顿第二定律的位移演化以及尺度化强度面，实现应变与断裂的双向耦合，并经动态与准静态实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统脆性断裂模型中应变与断裂之间的耦合往往是单向或不充分的，难以同时准确预测各向异性介质中的动态应力、材料强度以及强度与能量的竞争关系，因此需要建立一种能实现应变与断裂双向耦合的统一场理论框架。

Method: 提出四个核心概念：(1) 用于各向异性介质动态应力分析的新非局部变形梯度；(2) 源于本构定律的新型相场；(3) 基于牛顿第二定律的材料位移演化；(4) 将尺度化强度面引入本构定律以预测动态与准静态断裂模式。弹性软化由本构定律通过应变动力学控制，从而实现应变与断裂的双向耦合。

Result: 模拟结果与实验及其他数值方法对比验证了该方法在动态和准静态断裂分析中的有效性。模型能预测二氧化钛的材料强度、钠钙玻璃与二氧化钛中强度与能量的竞争，并捕捉钠钙玻璃与混凝土中的动态裂纹分叉现象。

Conclusion: 所提出的场理论能够有效统一描述脆性材料的动态与准静态断裂，准确再现强度、能量竞争及动态裂纹分叉等复杂行为，为脆性材料损伤与断裂预测提供了一个可靠的通用框架。

Abstract: A field theory is presented for predicting damage and fracture in brittle materials. Four concepts are used: a new nonlocal deformation gradient for dynamic stress analysis in anisotropic media, a new type of phase field coming from a constitutive law, the evolution of material displacement from Newton's second law, and a new incorporation of scaled strength surfaces into constitutive laws for predicting dynamic and quasi-static fracture patterns. The elastic softening is governed by the strain dynamics through the constitutive law and in this way we provide the two way coupling between strain and fracture. Simulations validating the approach for both dynamic and quasi-static fracture analysis are compared to experimental results and other simulation methods. The model is capable of predicting material strength in titania and the competition between strength and energy in soda lime glass and titana and captures dynamic crack branching in soda lime glass and concrete.

</details>

Motivation: 硼掺杂金刚石（BDD）可将电学功能与氮空位（NV）中心集成，但结静电学对 NV 电荷态稳定性的影响尚不清楚，因而需要厘清 BDD/氮掺杂金刚石结的平衡静电特性。

Method: 采用自洽 Poisson-Fermi 计算研究 BDD/氮掺杂金刚石结的平衡静电学，系统考察氮掺杂浓度、硼掺杂浓度、界面渐变尺度 w 以及 BDD 体电荷响应等因素对 NV$^{0/-}$ 电荷态边界与耗尽深度的影响。

Result: 氮掺杂衬底中存在两个不同的静电长度尺度：近界面的电离施主区主导能带弯曲并决定 NV$^{0/-}$ 电荷态边界，以及中性体中弱施主电离产生的更长屏蔽尾。NV$^-$ 耗尽深度强烈依赖氮浓度，但对硼掺杂的依赖较温和。当 N_B = 10^{21} cm^{-3} 时，界面渐变尺度 w 从 0.5 增至 8 nm 使耗尽深度改变约 22 nm，而 BDD 体电荷响应变化仅导致 1.27 nm 的偏移。

Abstract: Boron-doped diamond (BDD) enables the integration of electrical functionality with nitrogen-vacancy (NV) centers, but the influence of junction electrostatics on NV charge-state stability remains unclear. The results reveal two distinct electrostatic length scales in the nitrogen-doped substrate: a near-interface region of ionized donors that produces dominant band bending and determines the NV$^{0/-}$ charge-state boundary, and a much longer screening tail associated with weak donor ionization in the neutral bulk. At $N_B = 10^{21}$ cm$^{-3}$, varying the interface grading scale $w$ from 0.5 to 8 nm changes the depletion depth by approximately 22 nm, whereas variations in the modeled bulk BDD charge response produce a shift of only 1.27 nm. These results indicate that, under the conditions studied, the nitrogen donor concentration and local interface structure primarily govern the equilibrium NV charge-state profile, rather than the bulk electronic response of BDD alone.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 作者通过理论和实验论证铁电体中存在内禀的二次（非线性）贡献，该贡献源于铁电体的对称性破缺，并对不同厚度和不同曲率的铁电晶体进行分析，考察其应变梯度响应及电压输出特性。

As a consequence, ferroelectric crystals can generate second-harmonic voltage at twice the oscillation frequency of the bending.

</details>

Abstract: Swift heavy ion (SHI) irradiation generates nanoscale damage in materials, offering opportunities for controlled modification of structural properties. Understanding the fundamental processes governing damage formation in altered regions remains a longstanding challenge. This work explores how crystal structure influences swift heavy ion-induced damage formation and evolution in rutile and anatase TiO2. Combining transmission electron microscopy (TEM) and molecular dynamics simulations, we investigate how differences in atomic arrangement govern track morphology and structural recovery. Although irradiation produces amorphous tracks in both polymorphs, their morphologies differ considerably. In rutile, tracks develop pronounced diameter variations, forming hourglass-like structures, whereas in anatase they retain a relatively uniform cylindrical shape. Molecular dynamics simulations reveal that these differences arise from the distinct recrystallization behavior of the two crystal structures during rapid cooling, leading to different pathways of track evolution and recovery. Particular attention is paid to electron beam induced effects during TEM characterization, which can substantially modify the structure and apparent morphology of ion tracks. Prolonged electron beam exposure promotes the recrystallization of initially amorphous tracks and surface hillocks, accompanied by the development of Fresnel contrast. These findings highlight the importance of understanding beam-induced transformations for the reliable interpretation of TEM observations of radiation-damaged materials.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奇周期超胞（如3×3×5）常被用于五元等摩尔高熵钙钛矿氧化物的计算，但这类胞因无法容纳所有标准Glazer倾斜系统而约束了八面体倾斜。由于HEPO的八面体会响应局部阳离子环境，这种约束对计算结果的影响程度尚不明确，需要系统评估其造成的偏差。

Method: 采用机器学习原子间势，对13种HEPO对比奇周期超胞与倾斜可公度（tilt-commensurate）超胞的计算结果；在约1700原子的大胞中考察倾斜差异与能量差异，并将比较扩展到氧空位形成能等缺陷计算，最终归纳出等摩尔超胞的设计规则。

Result: 奇周期超胞在所有组合物中都产生更小、更不连贯的八面体倾斜，并在多数组合物中给出更高的能量，即使经过构型平均后偏差依然存在；约1700原子大胞中倾斜差异在全部组合物中持续存在。该偏差还延伸到缺陷：在强倾斜组合物中，奇周期胞给出更低的氧空位形成能。

Conclusion: 超胞周期性会系统性地偏置高熵钙钛矿氧化物的八面体倾斜与能量计算结果，因此计算中应避免使用约束倾斜的奇周期超胞；论文据此提出简单的等摩尔超胞设计规则，以解除倾斜约束并获得更可靠的结果。

Abstract: Odd-period supercells such as $3 \times 3 \times 5$ are often used for equimolar five-component compositions in computational studies of high-entropy perovskite oxides (HEPOs). However, such cells are known to constrain octahedral tilting because they cannot accommodate all standard tilt patterns (Glazer tilt systems). How strongly this constraint affects HEPOs, whose octahedra respond to local cation environments, remains unclear. Here we show that supercell periodicity systematically biases computed octahedral tilting and energetics. Using a machine-learning interatomic potential, we compared odd-period and tilt-commensurate cells for 13 HEPOs. Odd-period cells yielded smaller and less coherent tilts and higher energies, even after configurational averaging. In ~1,700-atom cells, tilt differences persisted in all compositions and energy differences in most. The bias extended to defects, with lower oxygen vacancy formation energies in odd-period cells for strongly tilted compositions. Based on these findings, a simple design rule for equimolar supercells is proposed to lift this constraint.

</details>

*Sakshi Patel,Paras Patel,Prafulla K. Jha*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GaAs是重要的III-V族半导体，其压力诱导相变是研究晶体结构、电子行为与相稳定性之间相互作用的关键途径，但此前的高压竞争相及其稳定序列尚不完全明确（例如此前提出的Imm2结构是否真正稳定）。

Method: 采用结构搜索算法与第一性原理计算结合，用PBEsol泛函进行静态焓计算；通过声子计算评估各相在代表性压力下的动力学稳定性，用弹性计算表征力学响应；并在准谐近似下纳入振动自由能，考察0–80 GPa、0–1000 K范围内的相竞争。

Result: 此前提出的Imm2结构位于计算的最低焓包络线之上。PBEsol静态焓预测相变序列为 F-43m → Cmcm → I-4m2 → C2/m → Amm2，转变压力分别约为12、14、34和69 GPa。初始的闪锌矿→Cmcm转变伴随约17.5%的体积收缩，并在PBEsol描述下发生金属化。声子计算支持五个相在代表性压力下的动力学稳定性。

Conclusion: 研究确定了GaAs的高压竞争结构，并将其相对能量与压缩GaAs的电子、振动和弹性响应联系起来，为理解该III-V半导体在高压下的相稳定性提供了依据。

Abstract: Pressure-induced transformations in GaAs offer a means to investigate the interplay between crystal structure, electronic behaviour, and phase stability in a technologically important III-V semiconductor. Here, we have used structure search algorithm combined with first-principles calculations and identified high-pressure competitive phases. The previously proposed $Imm2$ structure lies above the calculated minimum-enthalpy envelope. The static enthalpy calculations with the PBEsol functional predict the transition sequence $F\bar{4}3m \rightarrow Cmcm \rightarrow I\bar{4}m2 \rightarrow C2/m \rightarrow Amm2$, at approximately 12, 14, 34, and 69 GPa, respectively. The initial zinc blende to $Cmcm$ transition is accompanied by a volume reduction of approximately 17.5 % and metallization within the PBEsol description. Phonon calculations support the dynamical stability of all five phases at representative pressures, while elastic calculations characterize their mechanical response. Vibrational free energies are incorporated within the quasiharmonic approximation to examine phase competition over 0-80 GPa and 0-1000 K. These results identify competing high-pressure structures and connect their relative energetics with the electronic, vibrational, and elastic responses of compressed GaAs.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要一种在流行的PAW方法中准确评估外部电场诱导力的能力，以便在外部电场下对块体半导体和绝缘体进行第一性原理结构弛豫，进而研究线性、非线性电响应以及电场驱动的结构相变。

Result: 对半导体AlX（X=P、As、Sb）的计算结果显示，所得到的离子夹持和不夹持的介电常数、压电常数、二次介电极化率及Born有效电荷与DFPT结果及已有实验和理论数据吻合良好；AgNbO3中极化随电场变化的电滞回线与已有计算一致，成功复现电场驱动的反铁电-铁电相变。

Conclusion: 该PAW方法下电场诱导力计算能力准确可靠，为在外部电场下开展块体固体的第一性原理结构弛豫以及线性、非线性电响应和电场驱动结构相变研究提供了有效工具。

Abstract: We assess the performance of electric-field-induced forces for bulk solids within the projector-augmented wave method. This capability allows for first-principles structural relaxations of bulk semiconductors and insulators under external electric fields, thereby enabling the calculation of linear and nonlinear responses to electric fields as well as properties associated with electric-field-driven structural changes. The approach is first validated by presenting calculated ion-clamped and unclamped dielectric and piezoelectric constants, quadratic dielectric susceptibilities, and Born effective charges for semiconductors AlX (X=P, As and Sb), showing good agreement with density functional perturbation theory calculations as well as with available experimental and theoretical data. As a further demonstration, we examine the electric-field-driven antiferroelectric-to-ferroelectric phase transition in AgNbO$_3$, for which our computed polarization versus electric field hysteresis loop aligns with previous calculations.

</details>


### [92] [Magnetic History and Field Sweep Rate Effects on Transformation Hysteresis in $\mathrm{Ni}_{41}\mathrm{Co}_{9}\mathrm{Mn}_{31.5}\mathrm{Ga}_{18.5}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22442)

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该摘要研究了磁场循环诱导的亚稳马氏体态如何降低变磁形状记忆合金的磁滞，利用微秒脉冲磁化测量揭示其在微秒时间尺度上的相变动力学。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁致结构相变中的磁滞是变磁形状记忆合金应用的主要障碍。利用磁场循环产生亚稳马氏体态是降低磁滞的有前景途径，但其降低磁滞的机制尚不清楚。

Method: 采用微秒脉冲磁场磁化测量研究 Ni41Co9Mn31.5Ga18.5 的相变动力学，并与毫秒脉冲磁场测量结果进行对比，考察亚稳马氏体与初始马氏体对磁场扫描速率的响应差异。
