Abstract: Hysteresis in magnetic-field-induced structural phase transformations is a major obstacle to the application of metamagnetic shape memory alloys. One promising approach to reduce hysteresis is to utilize a metastable martensitic state generated by magnetic-field cycling. However, the mechanism by which the metastable state reduces hysteresis remains unclear. Here, microsecond pulsed-field magnetization measurements are used to investigate the transformation kinetics of $\mathrm{Ni}_{41}\mathrm{Co}_{9}\mathrm{Mn}_{31.5}\mathrm{Ga}_{18.5}$. Magnetic-field cycling reduces the hysteresis width by approximately 50-60%, demonstrating that substantial suppression persists on a microsecond timescale. Comparison with millisecond pulsed-field measurements suggests that the reverse martensitic transformation field is more sensitive to the field sweep rate for the metastable martensite than for the initial martensite. Possible explanations include enhanced thermal activation associated with a lower effective activation barrier and differences in the athermal contribution arising from different metastable martensitic microstructures.

</details>


### [93] [Efficient Machine Learning Interatomic Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22444)
*Yury Lysogorskiy,Anton Bochkarev,Ralf Drautz*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文以效率为核心，系统性地综述机器学习原子间势（MLIP），提出用原子簇展开（ACE）及其图推广（GRACE）作为统一表示框架，揭示各类架构的共性，并分析代价与精度的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLIP 的精度接近量子力学参考计算而成本极低，效率是其根本目的；然而此前尚无对高效 MLIP 策略的系统性综述。作者希望在统一基准上比较各类 MLIP，厘清架构多样性的本质，并给出训练成本如何随体系规模、模型复杂度变化的规律。

Method: 采用 ACE 及其图推广 GRACE 这一由单粒子函数乘积构成的规范（canonical）完备基展开，将所有 MLIP（及经典势如嵌入原子法 EAM、等变 transformer 等）统一表示。在此基础上分析非线性、层间耦合与优化方式的差异，并推导成本与系统尺寸、张量分解秩的关系，结合 Matbench Discovery 排行榜比较精度与代价。

Result: MLIP 的成本与体系规模呈线性关系，在固定张量分解秩时也与模型复杂度呈线性关系；架构多样性的主要来源是非线性处理、层耦合和优化策略，而非模型可表示的相互作用类型。在 Matbench Discovery 上，三至五层的模型精度接近更深模型；沿误差-速度 Pareto 前沿截断的同一基组，成本跨度超过两个数量级。

Conclusion: 效率只能在给定精度下讨论才有意义；训练数据最终决定 MLIP 的质量。ACE/GRACE 作为统一框架既能揭示各类模型的共性，也能指导在精度约束下的高效设计。

Abstract: Machine learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) reach an accuracy close to quantum mechanical reference calculations at a small fraction of the cost. Efficiency is the purpose of MLIPs, yet strategies for efficient MLIPs have not been reviewed systematically. This review takes efficiency as its guideline. To compare MLIPs on a common footing, we represent them in a complete basis expansion, the Atomic Cluster Expansion (ACE) and its graph generalization (GRACE). The expansion is canonical, built from products of single-particle functions. Classical interatomic potentials, such as the embedded-atom method, and many modern MLIPs, including equivariant transformers, can be represented within this expansion. Much of the apparent diversity of MLIP architectures therefore reflects how non-linearity, layer coupling and optimization are handled rather than which interactions the models can represent. We show how the cost of an MLIP becomes linear in system size and, at fixed rank of the tensor decomposition, in model complexity, and we discuss the role of machine learning, which provides non-linear parameterizations and optimization. Because efficiency is meaningful only at a given accuracy, we also compare accuracy and cost. On the Matbench Discovery leaderboard, models with three to five layers approach the accuracy of deeper models, and one basis, truncated along the Pareto front of error and speed, spans more than two orders of magnitude in cost. Ultimately, training data determine the quality of any MLIP.

</details>


### [94] [Burgers vectors from Voronoi topology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22488)
*Emanuel A. Lazar*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无参数、基于Voronoi拓扑的位错自动识别算法，可广泛适用于多种晶体结构，并以精确的Burgers矢量识别位错。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自动位错分析虽已是原子模拟的标准手段，但现有工具仅适用于少数晶体结构，缺乏一种通用、无需人工调参且不依赖几何模板的方法来识别广泛晶体中的位错及其Burgers矢量。

Method: 作者提出一种基于Voronoi拓扑的无参数算法。将两个与理想晶体匹配的Voronoi胞所共有的每个面视为晶体中的一步，在由这些胞构成的闭合回路中对这些步进行求和，即可精确得到Burgers矢量，整个过程不依赖几何模板。

Result: 在材料计划（Materials Project）中Voronoi胞具有宽裕稳定性的全部9,610种结构上插入位错进行测试，算法找出了所有位错并给出正确Burgers矢量。在原始分子动力学快照中，包括θ-Al₂Cu中的十一种位错类型以及直至熔点的体心立方铁，其返回的每个Burgers矢量均正确；仅在接近熔点时位错合并为一个团簇。

Conclusion: Voronoi拓扑为描述晶体、其缺陷及Burgers矢量提供了一种自然语言，所提算法能广泛、精确且无需参数地识别多种晶体中的位错。

Abstract: Automated dislocation analysis is standard in atomistic simulations, yet current tools are limited to a handful of crystal structures. We introduce an algorithm without adjustable parameters, based on Voronoi topology, that identifies dislocations in a broad class of crystals. Each face shared by two cells that match the ideal crystal is taken as a step in it, and summing these steps around closed circuits of such cells yields exact Burgers vectors, without geometric templates. Tested on dislocations inserted into all 9,610 Materials Project structures whose Voronoi cells are stable with a wide margin, the algorithm finds every dislocation with its correct Burgers vector. In raw molecular-dynamics snapshots, including eleven dislocation types in $θ$-Al$_2$Cu and bcc iron up to its melting point, every Burgers vector it returns is correct, although near melting the dislocations merge into one cluster. Voronoi topology thus provides a natural language in which a crystal, its defects, and their Burgers vectors are all described.

</details>


### [95] [Koopmans spectral functionals for excitons in materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22524)
*Miki Bonacci,Edward Linscott,Julian Geiger,Nicola Marzari,Nicola Colonna*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出 BSE@qKI 方法，用 Koopmans 兼容谱泛函的准粒子能量替代 GW 计算，以更低的成本准确表征激子，并在多个半导体及 CrI3 上验证了其精度与可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激子的精确第一性原理表征通常依赖 Bethe-Salpeter 方程（BSE）并结合计算成本高昂的 GW 方法获取的准粒子能量。作者希望找到一种能够避开设算力昂贵的 GW 步骤、同时保持乃至提升精度的替代方案，从而支持对复杂材料激子性质的系统研究。

Method: 作者提出 BSE@qKI 方法，即用 Koopmans 兼容谱泛函得到的准粒子能量替代 GW 步骤，再与 BSE 结合；并将该方法实现为一个自动化、可复现的 AiiDA 工作流。在一组基准半导体上评估其带隙和第一激子能量，并应用于层状磁性半导体 CrI3 计算吸收光谱。

Result: 在基准半导体上，BSE@qKI 复现实验带隙的平均绝对相对误差为 13%，第一激子能量为 4%；其中带隙误差与 BSE@G0W0 相当，而激发能精度明显优于 BSE@G0W0。对 CrI3 计算得到的吸收光谱复现了实验的主要特征。

Conclusion: BSE@qKI 可作为一种无 GW 的 BSE@G0W0 替代方案，用于对复杂材料的激子性质进行系统研究，兼具精度与计算可行性。

Abstract: Accurate first-principles characterization of excitons typically relies on the Bethe-Salpeter equation (BSE) combined with quasiparticle energies from computationally demanding GW calculations. Here, we replace the GW step with quasiparticle energies from Koopmans-compliant spectral functionals, an approach we call BSE@qKI, and implement it in an automated, reproducible AiiDA workflow. For a set of benchmark semiconductors, BSE@qKI reproduces experimental band gaps and first exciton energies with mean absolute relative errors of 13% and 4%, respectively, comparable to BSE@G0W0 for the gaps and markedly better for the excitation energies. We also apply the method to the layered magnetic semiconductor CrI3, obtaining an absorption spectrum that reproduces the main experimental features. These results establish BSE@qKI as a GW-free alternative to BSE@G0W0 for systematic studies of excitonic properties in complex materials.

</details>


### [96] [Solvent/Antisolvent-Driven Etching of 2D Layered Halide Perovskite Microcrystals toward Endo-Epitaxial Heterostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22529)
*Ajeet Singh,Sudhir Kumar Saini,Hriday Kumar Pal,Cara Elisabeth Bradsher,Sirous Khabbaz Abkenar,Roman Krahne,Liberato Manna*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种溶剂/反溶剂驱动的可控刻蚀策略，能在层状卤化物钙钛矿（LHP）单晶内部制备规则矩形空腔，并通过受限再生长形成内延异质结构，实现单晶内部异质界面的工程化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在单晶内部构筑内部界面极具挑战，尤其在层状卤化物钙钛矿这类软离子材料中，传统光刻方法难以实施，因此亟需一种适用于软离子材料单晶的内部界面工程方法。

Method: 采用溶剂/反溶剂驱动的可控化学刻蚀策略：刻蚀从晶体顶面开始，优先沿[100]和[010]晶向进行，形成规则矩形空腔；通过刻蚀时间、温度和刻蚀剂组成调控空腔尺寸；反应可中断并恢复，实现逐步空腔演化；随后以空腔为受限模板，再生长组成不同的LHP，并通过顺序加入前驱体控制内部再生长程度。

Result: 成功在LHP微晶中制备出规则矩形空腔，并通过受限再生长获得具有设计内部结构的内延异质结构；该策略适用于多种LHP组成和反溶剂体系，空腔尺寸和内部再生长程度均可调控。

Conclusion: 该策略建立了一条通过可控化学刻蚀与受限生长在单晶钙钛矿中工程化内部异质界面的新途径，为软离子材料单晶内部界面的构筑提供了通用方法。

Abstract: Engineering internal interfaces within single crystals remains challenging, particularly in soft ionic materials such as layered halide perovskites (LHPs), for which conventional lithographic approaches are difficult to implement. Here we report a solvent/antisolvent-driven strategy for controlled etching of LHP microcrystals, producing well-defined rectangular cavities. Etching initiates at the top surface and proceeds preferentially along the crystallographic [100] and [010] directions. Cavity dimensions are tuned through etching time, temperature and etchant composition. The reaction can be interrupted and resumed, enabling stepwise cavity evolution through successive etching cycles. The resulting cavities serve as confined templates for regrowth of compositionally distinct LHPs, yielding endo-epitaxial heterostructures with designed internal architectures. The approach extends across diverse LHP compositions and antisolvent systems, while sequential precursor addition controls the extent of internal regrowth. This strategy establishes a route to engineering internal heterointerfaces in single-crystal perovskites through controlled chemical etching and confined growth.

</details>


### [97] [First-order correction to the self-consistent harmonic approximation free energy: Impact on the negative thermal expansion and phonon dynamics in ScF$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22538)
*Jincheng Yue,Manex Alkorta,Đorđe Dangić,Yue-Wen Fang,Ion Errea*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文推导出自洽谐波近似（SCHA）变分优化高斯态之上的一阶后变分修正，该修正来自最低阶非零正规序残差顶点的二阶涨落。在ScF₃中，变分优化已能解释大部分热膨胀交叉温度变化，而连接的非高斯关联虽对交叉温度影响温和，但能显著影响自由能导数与压强重映射的立方局域稳定性极限，并对高温柔性模动力学产生重要修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自洽谐波近似与自洽声子理论用变分优化的高斯密度矩阵处理强非谐晶体，能捕捉显著的声子重整化，但排除了连接的非高斯自由能关联。这些非高斯关联对自由能导数及结构不稳定性附近的软模动力学可能至关重要，因此需要推导并量化后变分修正。

Method: 在优化高斯态周围对残差相互作用做正规序（normal ordering），证明线性与二次顶点在变分驻点处消失，仅剩涨落修饰的立方相互作用。其二阶真空收缩给出自由能修正，两点收缩给出动力学气泡（bubble）自能。将该框架应用于ScF₃，在完整Born-Oppenheimer势上做变分优化，并对比裸立方顶点与涨落修饰顶点的结果。

Result: 在ScF₃中，完整势上的变分优化解释了负/正热膨胀交叉温度相对四阶势自洽声子理论的大部分偏移；连接修正仅温和改变交叉温度，但其体积导数显著移动压强重映射的立方局域稳定性极限。使用裸立方顶点更接近热力学测量，但系统性地低估高温区边界倾斜模能量；涨落修饰相互作用给出的热力学修正较温和，但声子能量与实验更吻合。

Conclusion: 连接的非高斯关联能够显著影响结构不稳定性附近的自由能导数与软模动力学；优化高斯态应作为超越随机自洽谐波近似、构造残差相互作用的自然参考态。

Abstract: Strongly anharmonic crystals are often treated using the self-consistent harmonic approximation with variationally optimized Gaussian density matrices or the related self-consistent phonon theory. Both approaches capture substantial phonon renormalization but exclude connected non-Gaussian free-energy correlations. Here, we derive the first-order post-variational correction generated by the lowest non-vanishing normal-ordered residual vertex. Normal ordering the residual interaction around the optimized state shows that the linear and quadratic vertices vanish at the stationary variational solution, leaving a fluctuation-dressed cubic interaction whose second-order vacuum and two-point contractions generate the free-energy correction and the dynamical bubble self-energy, respectively. In ScF$_3$, the variational optimization on the full Born-Oppenheimer potential accounts for most of the shift in the thermal-expansion crossover temperature between negative and positive values relative to self-consistent phonon theory with a fourth-order potential. The connected correction changes the crossover modestly, but its volume derivative appreciably shifts the pressure-remapped cubic local-stability limit. Using the bare cubic vertex appears to improve agreement with the thermodynamic measurements, yet it systematically underestimates the measured zone-boundary tilt-mode energies at high temperatures. The fluctuation-dressed interaction gives more moderate thermodynamic corrections but yields phonon energies in better agreement with experiments. These results show that connected non-Gaussian correlations can significantly affect free-energy derivatives and soft-mode dynamics near a structural instability, and identify the optimized Gaussian state as the natural reference for constructing residual interactions beyond the stochastic self-consistent harmonic approximation.

</details>


### [98] [Engineering antiferromagnetic (LaVO3)m/(PrVO3)n superlattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22861)
*A. Benedit-Cardenas,G. Masset,M. Bugnet,M. Martirosyan,G. Steciuk,S. Migot,J. Ghanbaja,L. Pasquier,K. Dumesnil,Q. M. Ramasse,O. Copie*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文通过MBE外延生长(LaVO3)m/(PrVO3)n反铁磁超晶格，实现了原子尺度结构畸变的精确调控，揭示了界面驱动的结构畸变与磁性、轨道杂化之间的耦合关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 钙钛矿氧化物中氧八面体旋转、阳离子位移等结构畸变与磁学性质密切相关，但如何在原子尺度精确调控这些畸变及其与磁性的关联仍是挑战。作者希望通过LaVO3/PrVO3超晶格这一模型体系，系统研究界面驱动的结构畸变如何传播并影响反铁磁性质。

Method: 采用分子束外延(MBE)在(001)取向SrTiO3衬底上实现(LaVO3)m/(PrVO3)n超晶格的逐层精确生长；利用X射线衍射(XRD)与倒空间图(RSM)确认外延应变状态与晶体质量；使用高分辨扫描透射电子显微镜(HR-STEM)解析层分辨的原子位移；通过层分辨光谱测量分析La/Pr 5d与O 2p轨道的杂化，并以阳离子位移和轨道杂化作为间接探针，可视化氧八面体旋转与反极性稀土位移之间的三线性耦合。

Result: La3+与Pr3+表现出受超晶格周期调制的反极性位移；LaVO3与PrVO3交替堆叠产生独特的反铁磁转变温度，且可通过调节两者相对比例进行调控，即便各组分材料本身具有不同的自旋有序温度。层分辨光谱显示La/Pr 5d与O 2p轨道杂化同样受调制，并与氧八面体旋转直接相关。立方SrTiO3衬底与正交钒酸盐层之间的界面氧八面体连通性显著影响结构畸变：畸变在界面附近几乎被抑制，并在数个晶胞内逐渐弛豫。

Conclusion: 该研究证明人工超晶格可在原子尺度上工程化调控结构畸变，借助应变-畸变关系为钙钛矿氧化物磁性性质的调控提供了新途径。

Abstract: In this study, we demonstrate the atomic-scale engineering of antiferromagnetic (LaVO3)m/(PrVO3)n superlattices grown by MBE on (001)-oriented SrTiO3 substrates. Our work achieved a precise layer-by-layer control, revealing how interface-driven structural distortions can be systematically tuned. X-ray diffraction and reciprocal space mapping confirm both the epitaxial strain state and the high crystalline quality of the superlattices. High-resolution scanning transmission electron microscopy further uncovers layer-resolved atomic displacements, where La3+ and Pr3+ exhibit antipolar motions modulated by the superlattice periodicity. The alternate stackings of LaVO3 and PrVO3 yield a unique antiferromagnetic transition temperature, which can be tuned by adjusting their relative proportions. This behavior exists despite the distinct spin ordering temperatures observed in the constituent materials. Layer-resolved spectroscopic measurements reveal that the hybridization of La and Pr 5d orbitals with O 2p orbitals is also modulated and directly correlated to the oxygen octahedra rotations. By determining the orbital hybridization and mapping the cation displacements as proxies, we can visualize indirectly the trilinear coupling between oxygen octahedra rotations and antipolar rare earth displacements. Notably, the interfacial oxygen octahedra connectivity between cubic SrTiO3 substrate and orthorhombic vanadate layers significantly influences the structural distortions. These distortions, almost quenched near the interface, relax over few unit cells, demonstrating how strain and interfacial effects propagate through the superlattice. This study illustrates how artificial superlattices can be engineered to control structural distortions at the atomic scale, offering a pathway to tune the magnetic properties through strain-distortion relationships in perovskite oxides.

</details>


### [99] [Targeted material doping to enable the formation and coherent propagation of Laser Induced Periodic Surface Structures (LIPSS): the ZnO paradigm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22573)
*Gonzalo Gomez-Munoz,Rocio Ariza,Juan Francisco Ramos-Justicia,Fatima Cabello Pardos,Antonio Pablo Pérez-Marín,Jose Gonzalo,Belén Sotillo,Binbing Xia,Paloma Fernandez,Javier Solis*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文研究了初始自由载流子密度如何决定低空间频率激光诱导周期表面结构（LSF-LIPSS）的形成与相干传播，以掺Yb³⁺的ZnO薄膜为范例，证明通过掺杂提高室温载流子密度（ne>10¹⁹ cm⁻³）可实现大面积、高质量LSF-LIPSS的相干传播，从而在宏观区域产生显著的电学各向异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ZnO作为一种多功能透明导电氧化物（TCO），在生物医学、光子学、传感和光催化等领域有广泛应用，许多应用可受益于大面积、电学各向异性的表面。然而，实现大面积、高质量且相干传播的LSF-LIPSS仍受限于材料的初始自由载流子密度及ZnO的高熔点、烧蚀机制等因素，因此需要探究载流子密度对LSF-LIPSS形成与传播的调控作用。

Method: 作者采用脉冲激光沉积（PLD）制备掺入少量Yb³⁺作为施主掺杂剂的ZnO薄膜，以提高室温载流子密度和电导率，同时保持纯ZnO的功能特性。随后使用1030 nm、350 fs、500 kHz高重复频率的激光，以超过1 m/s的扫描速度对薄膜进行辐照，系统考察了初始载流子密度、衬底热导率、薄膜厚度及脉冲持续时间对大面积LSF-LIPSS形态（尤其是避免薄膜开裂）的影响，并对结构进行优化。

Result: 当室温载流子密度ne>10¹⁹ cm⁻³时，成功实现了LSF-LIPSS的相干传播。研究发现ZnO的烧蚀机制（LIPSS谷底处ZnO升华）及其高熔点使衬底热导率、薄膜厚度和辐照脉冲持续时间对大面积LSF-LIPSS的形态起关键作用，尤其在避免薄膜开裂方面至关重要。优化后的结果证实了自由载流子密度在大面积高质量LSF-LIPSS传播中的核心作用。

Conclusion: 通过掺杂提高自由载流子密度可实现掺杂ZnO大面积高质量LSF-LIPSS的相干传播，这些结构能够方便地在宏观区域产生显著的电学各向异性，沿LIPSS方向的电导率比横向高出数倍，为其在光电及功能器件中的应用提供了新途径。

Abstract: This work explores how the initial free carrier density governs the formation and coherent propagation of Low Spatial Frequency Laser-Induced Periodic Surface Structures (LSF-LIPSS). As a paradigm we have analyzed the behavior of ad-hoc-doped ZnO films. ZnO is a multifunctional Transparent Conducting Oxide (TCO) with many scientific and technologic uses including applications in biomedicine, photonics, sensing or photocatalysis. Many of them would benefit from the use of large area, electrically anisotropic surfaces. By introducing small amounts of Yb3+ as donor dopant in ZnO films grown by Pulse Laser Deposition (PLD), we have substantially improved its carrier density at room temperature (RT) and its electrical conductivity while preserving the functional properties of pure ZnO. For RT carrier densities ne > 1019 cm-3, coherent propagation of LSF-LIPSS has been successfully achieved upon irradiation with 350 fs laser pulses at 1030 nm at high repetition rate (500 kHz) and for laser scanning speeds well above 1 m/s. However, the ablation mechanism (ZnO sublimation at the LIPSS valleys) and the large melting temperature of ZnO, make the thermal conductivity of the substrate, the film thickness and the irradiation pulse duration to play a crucial role in determining the morphology of the LSF-LIPPS propagated over large areas, especially in avoiding film cracking. The results achieved upon optimization fully confirm the role of free carrier density in the large-scale propagation of high quality LSF-LIPSS in doped ZnO and enable using these structures to easily generate large electrical anisotropies over macroscopic regions with conductivities along the LIPSS several times larger than transversally.

</details>


### [100] [Prior-guided adaptive local refinement of foundation interatomic potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22626)
*Yunlong Wang,Jianzhen Ma,Yusu Zhang,Jiahang Li,Qinqiu Cheng,Junjie Yuan,Wenrui Yan,Yucheng Zhang,Chen Yu,Weibin Chu,Hongxia Hao,Jielan Li*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出 PARS 方法，在分子动力学中通过对比冻结表征与预训练环境原型来识别基础原子间势支持薄弱的构型，将其重构为紧凑周期胞进行第一性原理标注与迭代精化，无需初始任务标签或模型集成，并在相稳定性、表面迁移与空位团簇等任务中显著提升精度，支持将精化势蒸馏为轻量模型用于大规模模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础原子间势虽能跨材料模拟，但在特定任务中可能存在精度不足，通常需要补充第一性原理数据；然而当相关局部构型出现在大规模模拟胞中时，获取这些数据非常困难。

Method: 提出 PARS（Prior-guided Adaptive Refinement Scheme）：在分子动力学过程中将局部环境的冻结表征与预训练环境的原型进行比较，识别模型支持薄弱的构型，无需初始任务标签或模型集成；将选定构型重构为紧凑周期胞，用于第一性原理标注和迭代精化，并可将精化后的势蒸馏为紧凑势。

Result: 在相稳定性、表面迁移和空位团簇等应用中提升了目标性质精度，Ti 空位团簇能量每空位平均绝对误差较预训练模型降低 81%；对 Si/Ge，用 800 个含 10–128 原子的标注胞精化基础势后蒸馏为紧凑势，在约 362,880 原子模拟中预测的界面热阻与直接用 DFT 数据训练的势一致。

Conclusion: 展示了通过局部参考计算将基础势适配到目标应用的有效途径，证明 PARS 能在无初始标签的条件下高效识别并精化弱支持构型，实现大规模模拟中可靠的性质预测。

Abstract: Foundation interatomic potentials enable simulations across diverse materials, but task-specific inaccuracies can require additional first-principles data. Obtaining these data is challenging when the relevant local environments arise in large simulation cells. Here we introduce the Prior-guided Adaptive Refinement Scheme (PARS), which identifies weakly supported environments during molecular dynamics by comparing their frozen representations with prototypes of pretraining environments, without requiring initial task labels or model ensembles. Selected environments are reconstructed as compact periodic cells for first-principles labelling and iterative refinement. Applications to phase stability, surface migration and vacancy clustering show improvements in target properties, including an 81% reduction in the mean absolute error of Ti vacancy-cluster energies per vacancy relative to the pretrained model. For Si/Ge, a foundation potential refined using 800 labelled cells containing 10-128 atoms is subsequently distilled into a compact potential without additional first-principles training labels. In simulations containing approximately 362,880 atoms, the distilled model predicts an interfacial thermal resistance consistent with that of a potential trained directly on density-functional theory data. These results demonstrate a route for adapting foundation potentials to target applications through local reference calculations.

</details>


### [101] [Computational Modeling of Sublattice Magnetization in CoTb/Co/CoTb Magnetic Multilayer Nanojunctions Between Cobalt Leads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22644)
*Sushita Sharma,Vinod Ashokan*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文研究夹在半无限钴电极之间的 CoTb/Co/CoTb 铁磁纳米结构的亚晶格磁行为，通过 Ising 有效场理论拟合实验磁化曲线确定 Co 与 Tb 的交换参数，再将其引入平均场理论计算各原子层的亚晶格磁化强度，揭示局域磁性、成分依赖的补偿行为，为自旋电子学和磁子输运研究提供依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 钴电极夹持的超薄层状铁磁结构对磁子输运与磁放大效应至关重要，但目前尚无第一性原理或密度泛函理论研究该类多层体系的亚晶格磁化；缺乏对局域层面（自旋位点尺度）磁现象和铁磁补偿行为的系统认识，制约了自旋电子器件的发展。

Method: 采用 Ising 自旋有效场理论（EFT）拟合实验测得的温度依赖磁化曲线，确定元素 Co（JCo-Co）与 Tb（JTb-Tb）的磁交换参数及各自居里温度；再将所得交换参数引入平均场理论（MFT），逐层、逐晶格位点计算夹在半无限钴引线之间的 CoTb/Co/CoTb 结构的亚晶格磁化强度，针对不同共晶成分（尤其 c ≤ 0.5）分析自旋位点参数、磁矩、磁化强度及铁磁补偿行为。

Result: 获得了 Co 与 Tb 的磁交换常数和居里温度；计算出多层铁磁纳米结构中每层原子及其晶格位点的亚晶格磁化强度，揭示了成分变化（c ≤ 0.5）下的局域磁矩、磁化与铁磁补偿行为，结果增强了对超薄层状铁磁结构局域磁现象及其在钴电极限制下动力学的理解。

Conclusion: 该工作中建立的 Ising EFT 拟合实验确定交换参数、再馈入 MFT 计算亚晶格磁化强度的通用方法，填补了此类多层体系缺乏第一性原理/DFT 研究的空白，为磁子输运和磁放大效应研究提供关键输入，对自旋电子技术的发展至关重要，并可推广至其他纳米磁系统。

Abstract: In this work, we study the sublattice nano-magnetic behavior of CoTb/Co/CoTb sandwiched between semi-infinite cobalt leads. To perform the analysis, we use the Ising spin-based effective field theory (EFT), which enables us to determine the relevant magnetic exchange parameters for elemental cobalt JCo-Co and terbium JTb-Tb. The exchange interaction parameters are determined by fitting the magnetization calculated using the Ising effective-field theory to the experimentally measured temperature-dependent magnetization curves. This fitting procedure yields the exchange constants for Co and Tb, along with their respective Curie temperatures. We use the magnetic exchange parameter obtained using Ising EFT-based method is then seeded on to mean field theory (MFT) to calculate the sublattice magnetizations of CoTb/Co/CoTb arranged between semi-infinite cobalt leads. The sublattice magnetizations for every atomic layer and corresponding lattice sites in the layered ferrimagnetic nanostructures are computed separately. This allows us to analyze spin-site-specific parameters, magnetic moments, magnetization, and ferrimagnetic compensation behavior for varying eutectic compositions, particularly for \(c \leq 0.5\). The results give enhanced insight into magnetic phenomena at the local level and their general relevance in the ultra-thin layered ferrimagnetic structure dynamics confined between semi-infinite cobalt electrodes. These results are needed for the understanding of magnon transport phenomena and consequent magnetic amplification effects and are crucial to the development of spintronic technologies. Most importantly, to date, no first-principles or density functional theory research exists for sublattice magnetization in such multilayered systems. The versatile approach established in this work can also be applied to other nanostructured magnetic systems

</details>


### [102] [VQkit: A Unified and Extensible Framework for VASP and Quantum ESPRESSO Pre- and Post-Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22664)
*Rajkamal Anand,Seunghwan Kwon,Gun Jin Yun*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: VQkit 是一个基于 Python 的计算工具包，用于统一 VASP 与 Quantum ESPRESSO 密度泛函理论计算的前后处理，支持跨代码结构转换、自动输入生成及标准化性质分析，并通过弹性性质基准验证其可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有第一性原理计算工作流被多种软件（VASP、QE）割裂，不同代码的输入输出格式和数据结构不一致，导致结构转换、输入制备与结果后处理繁琐且易出错。作者旨在通过一个统一的 Python 框架消除这种工作流碎片化，提供一致的前后处理环境。

Method: VQkit 采用晶格矢量、原子坐标、化学元素和位置约束的统一表示，实现跨代码结构转换、几何操作、晶体学重构及代码特定输入生成。前处理涵盖对称性相关的倒空间路径生成、原子约束和输入自动制备；后处理包括收敛性检查、总能与费米能级提取、能带与态密度分析、轨道分辨电子结构处理、电荷密度分析及材料应用性质评估。通过 HfC、ZrC、ZrO2、Ti 和金刚石的弹性性质基准测试验证实现正确性。

Result: 弹性性质基准测试表明，VQkit 与 VASPKIT 在 VASP 计算上结果高度一致，而对应的 QE 计算结果存在差异；这表明 VQkit 能可靠复现 VASPKIT 的 VASP 处理流程，同时反映出不同代码间（VASP 与 QE）结果差异及其对前后处理一致性的影响。

Conclusion: VQkit 通过在同一计算环境中整合跨代码互操作性、自动化模拟制备、标准化后处理和定量性质评估，有效降低了工作流碎片化，为第一性原理计算提供了一致、可复现的前后处理方案。

Abstract: VQkit is a Python-based computational toolkit developed to streamline the pre- and post-processing of density functional theory calculations performed using Vienna Ab initio Simulation Package (VASP) and Quantum ESPRESSO (QE). The framework employs a unified representation of lattice vectors, atomic coordinates, chemical species, and positional constraints, enabling cross-code structure conversion, geometry manipulation, crystallographic reconstruction, and code-specific input generation. Its pre-processing capabilities include symmetry-dependent reciprocal-space path generation, atomic constraints, and automated preparation of simulation inputs. The post-processing framework provides calculation convergence inspection, extraction of total energies and Fermi levels, electronic band-structure and density-of-states analysis, orbital-resolved electronic-structure processing, charge-density analysis, and evaluation of application-specific materials properties. The computational implementation is evaluated through elastic-property benchmarks for HfC, ZrC, ZrO2, Ti, and diamond, showing close agreement between VQkit and VASPKIT for VASP-based calculations, with differences observed in the corresponding QE results. By integrating cross-code interoperability, automated simulation preparation, standardized post-processing, and quantitative property evaluation within a common computational environment, VQkit reduces workflow fragmentation and facilitates consistent pre- and post-processing of first-principles calculations.

</details>


### [103] [Altermagnetism-induced magneto-optical effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22750)
*Jun Ōiké,Robert Peters,Koki Shinada*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出"八极磁光效应"（octupolar MO effects），其来源于磁八极序并通过空间色散产生。该效应无需自旋轨道耦合即可在共线磁体中出现，且可在传统磁光效应被对称性禁止的反铁磁体（如d波交错磁体RuO₂、KV₂Se₂O，部分g/i波交错磁体如CrSb，以及非共面反铁磁体MnTe₂、全进全出磁序的烧绿石铱氧化物）中无外磁场地产生。数值计算表明其可达μrad量级的法拉第旋转（100 nm厚样品、可见光波段），且相反磁畴间符号反转，可用于磁畴成像；并与f求和规则关联，可定量估算自旋磁八极矩。该工作将磁光效应扩展到包括交错磁体在内的广泛反铁磁体，开辟了无磁场探测磁八极序的光学途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交错磁体在零净磁化下破缺时间反演对称性，可实现时间反演奇响应，但传统磁光效应通常源于铁磁中的磁偶极序，在交错磁体中普遍缺失，因此磁光效应并非在交错磁体间普遍存在。作者旨在突破这一限制，寻找一种不必依赖磁偶极序、且不被对称性禁止的磁光效应机制。

Method: 作者从磁八极序出发，通过空间色散机制建立八极磁光效应理论框架。该效应源于磁八极序，与依赖磁偶极序的传统磁光效应起源不同，因而在共线磁体中即使无自旋轨道耦合也可出现，并在传统磁光效应被对称性禁止的反铁磁体中无外磁场即可产生。文中通过对称性与候选材料分析判定适用体系，并进行数值计算给出法拉第旋转量级与磁畴依赖行为，同时建立与f求和规则的定量联系以提取自旋磁八极矩。

Result: 八极磁光效应可出现在包括RuO₂、KV₂Se₂O等d波交错磁体，CrSb等部分g/i波交错磁体，以及MnTe₂、全进全出磁序烧绿石铱氧化物等非共面反铁磁体中。数值计算显示100 nm厚样品在可见光波段可产生μrad量级的法拉第旋转，且相反磁畴间旋转角符号反转，可用于磁畴成像。该效应还联系f求和规则，可由光学测量定量估算自旋磁八极矩。

Conclusion: 该工作将磁光效应推广到包含交错磁体在内的一大类反铁磁体，并提出一种无需外磁场的、探测磁八极序的光学手段，为磁畴成像与磁八极矩定量表征开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Altermagnets break time-reversal symmetry despite their zero net magnetization, enabling time-reversal-odd responses while retaining the advantages of antiferromagnets. However, magneto-optical (MO) effects are not universal among altermagnets because MO effects typically arise from magnetic dipole order (ferromagnetism), which is generally absent in altermagnets. In this work, we address this limitation by establishing octupolar MO effects, which arise from magnetic octupole order through spatial dispersion. Owing to this distinct origin, octupolar MO effects do not necessarily require spin-orbit coupling even in collinear magnets and can arise without an external magnetic field in antiferromagnets where conventional MO effects are symmetry-forbidden. Such antiferromagnets include $d$-wave altermagnets such as RuO$_2$ and KV$_2$Se$_2$O, certain $g$- and $i$-wave altermagnets, including CrSb, as well as noncoplanar antiferromagnets such as MnTe$_2$ and pyrochlore iridates with all-in-all-out magnetic order. From an experimental perspective, octupolar MO effects can reach detectable magnitudes and offer potential for magnetic-domain imaging, as supported by numerical calculations showing $μ$rad-scale Faraday rotation for a 100-nm-thick sample in the visible-light range and a sign reversal between opposite domains. Furthermore, octupolar MO effects are linked to an $f$-sum rule, enabling a quantitative estimate of the spin magnetic octupole moment from optical measurements. These results extend the scope of MO effects to a broad class of antiferromagnets, including altermagnets, and open a field-free optical route to exploring magnetic octupole order.

</details>


### [104] [Interfacial electrostatics: From polar insulators to charged-layer metals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22776)
*Edith Simmen,Nicholas C. Bristowe,Philippe Ghosez,Nicola A. Spaldin*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文总结了极不连续性的理论框架，并探讨了稀土镍酸盐（如SmNiO3）这类具有形式带电层的金属与铁电体BaTiO3界面处出现的“类极不连续性”行为，尝试用已有的绝缘体间极不连续性与金属界面屏蔽概念加以解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典极不连续性框架适用于绝缘薄膜与真空、非极性绝缘体或金属的界面，但新观测发现含有形式带电层的金属（如稀土镍酸盐）界面也表现出类似行为，这对现有理论图景构成挑战，需要厘清并评估现有理论能否解释。

Method: 作者系统总结用于定义和量化界面电荷、层极化以及屏蔽效应的理论框架（涵盖LaAlO3/SrTiO3、BaTiO3/SrRuO3、BiFeO3/SrRuO3三个参考体系），随后分析SmNiO3（具有形式带电(001)原子层的金属）与铁电体BaTiO3界面上归因于形式电荷极不连续性的实验特征。

Result: 研究表明，为绝缘体间极不连续性和金属界面屏蔽所发展的概念能够合理阐释所报道的效应，同时明确了现有理解在这些体系中仍存在的未解问题。

Conclusion: 经典的极不连续性与屏蔽概念可推广用于解释含形式带电层的金属/铁电体界面行为，但这类新体系仍有若干开放问题有待进一步研究，促使理论框架的扩展与完善。

Abstract: Polar discontinuities are typically described as arising from charged atomic planes, a spontaneous polarization, or a combination, in insulating films interfaced with vacuum, nonpolar insulators or metals. The resulting electrostatics are well established for systems such as LaAlO$_3$/SrTiO$_3$, BaTiO$_3$/SrRuO$_3$, and BiFeO$_3$/SrRuO$_3$. However, recent observations of "polar-discontinuity-like" behavior at interfaces in which the metal contains formally charged layers (e.g., rare-earth nickelates) challenge this picture. In this work, we summarize the theoretical framework used to define and quantify interface charge, layer polarization, and screening in the three reference systems above, and then discuss signatures attributed to the formal-charge polar discontinuity arising when SmNiO$_3$, a metal with formally charged (001) layers, is interfaced with ferroelectric BaTiO$_3$. We show how concepts developed for polar discontinuities between two insulators and for screening at metallic interfaces can rationalize the reported effects and identify where our understanding of these systems leaves open questions.

</details>


### [105] [Canonical spin glass behavior and memory effects in van der Waals magnet (Fe0.55Ni0.45)3GaTe2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22781)
*P. Biswal,Kakan Deb,Nitesh Kumar,D. Samal,Prabhat Mandal*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文在单晶 Ni 掺杂范德华磁体 (Fe0.55Ni0.45)3GaTe2 中系统论证了典型自旋玻璃行为及其非平衡动力学，通过交流磁化率与直流磁化测量确认冻结温度 T_f≈45.5 K。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究 Ni 掺杂范德华磁体 (Fe0.55Ni0.45)3GaTe2 中是否产生典型自旋玻璃态，并厘清其玻璃态来源于协同自旋-自旋相互作用而非超顺磁性，为范德华磁性材料中的阻挫与无序效应提供实验证据。

Method: 采用详细的交流磁化率与直流磁化测量。直流磁化随温度变化显示出强磁不可逆性；交流磁化率峰随频率升高向高温移动，并估算冻结温度每十倍频程的相对偏移 δT_f=0.0072。用幂律动力学标度分析频率依赖的冻结温度得到单自旋翻转弛豫时间 τ_0 和临界指数 zν，并以 Vogel-Fulcher 定律拟合得到 Vogel-Fulcher 温度 T_0 与激活能 E_a/k_B。通过磁弛豫与记忆效应实验研究非平衡动力学，特别是零场冷磁化的记忆效应，并利用正负温度循环下的非对称弛豫响应判别记忆效应的理论模型。

Result: 确认冻结温度 T_f≈45.5 K；δT_f=0.0072；幂律拟合给出 τ_0=2×10^-13 s、zν=8.1；Vogel-Fulcher 拟合给出 T_0=43 K、E_a/k_B=81 K，且 E_a/k_BT_0=1.88。上述特征参数均处于典型自旋玻璃（canonical spin glass）范围。零场冷磁化的记忆效应表明玻璃态源于协同自旋-自旋相互作用而非超顺磁性；磁弛豫对正负温度循环的非对称响应支持记忆效应的分层（hierarchical）模型而非液滴（droplet）模型。

Conclusion: (Fe0.55Ni0.45)3GaTe2 表现出典型的自旋玻璃态与非平衡动力学行为，其特征参数符合典型自旋玻璃的判据；玻璃行为起源于协同自旋-自旋相互作用，其记忆效应更符合分层模型。

Abstract: We demonstrate the canonical spin-glass behavior and non-equilibrium dynamics in a single-crystalline Ni-doped van der Waals magnet (Fe0.55Ni0.45)3GaTe2 with a freezing temperature $T_f \approx 45.5$ K from detailed ac susceptibility and dc magnetization measurements. The temperature-dependent dc magnetization exhibits strong magnetic irreversibility and the ac susceptibility shows that the peak at $T_f$ shifts towards higher temperatures with increase in ac frequency, indicating the presence of a spin glass state. The relative shift of the freezing temperature $δT_f$ per decade of frequency in ac susceptibility is estimated to be 0.0072. Dynamic scaling analysis of the frequency-dependent freezing temperature using the power law reveals the single spin flip relaxation time $τ_0=2\times10^{-13}$ s and critical exponent $zν=8.1$, while the Vogel-Fulcher law yields Vogel-Fulcher temperature $T_0=43$ K and activation energy $E_a/k_B=81$ K with $E_a/k_BT_0=1.88$. The obtained values of these characteristic parameters lie well within the range associated with a canonical spin glass. The detailed non-equilibrium dynamics study, involving the magnetic relaxation and memory effect, further confirms the glassy behavior. The memory effect in zero-field-cooled magnetization establishes that the glassy state arises from the cooperative spin-spin interaction and not from superparamagnetism. The asymmetric response of magnetic relaxation with respect to negative and positive temperature cycle favors the hierarchical model of memory effects over the droplet model.

</details>


### [106] [From a High-T$\rm_C$ Spin-Gapless Semiconductor to a Spin-Glass: The Role of Disorder in NiMnCrAl](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22784)
*Arka Patra,Tufan Roy,Nilofar Kurawle,Tamaghna Maitra,Souvik Banerjee,Mantu Modak,Shuvankar Gupta,Sudip Chakraborty,Soumya Bhowmik,Papri Dasgupta,Rajashri Urkude,Biplab Ghosh,D. Bhattacharyya,A. Sundaresan,Sudhindra Rayaprol,Masafumi Shirai,Chandan Mazumdar*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究结合第一性原理计算与结构、磁性、输运测量，发现NiMnCrAl四元Heusler合金的化学无序会破坏其理想的自旋无能隙半导体态和亚铁磁序，转而形成玻璃态磁性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋无能隙半导体（spin-gapless semiconductor）是重要的自旋电子学候选材料，但其有序态对真实的化学无序高度敏感。作者旨在阐明无序如何影响NiMnCrAl的电子结构与磁性，以评估该类材料的实际可用性。

Method: 采用密度泛函理论与平均场分析预测有序Y型结构的基态性质；用多晶样品进行X射线衍射、中子衍射和扩展X射线吸收精细结构（EXAFS）表征原子占位无序；通过磁化率、交流磁化率、弛豫及记忆效应测量磁性行为；并辅以电阻率与Seebeck系数输运测量；交换相互作用计算用于揭示磁阻挫的微观来源。

Result: 有序Y型结构理论上为亚铁磁自旋无能隙半导体，磁矩1.99 μB/f.u.，平均场居里温度约1300 K。实际多晶NiMnCrAl形成单一平均立方相，但存在以B2型为主的显著反位无序及跨亚晶格混合。材料在700 K以下无长程亚铁磁转变，却在约37 K出现宽磁异常，伴随频率依赖的交流磁化率、慢拉伸指数弛豫及场冷/零场冷记忆效应，确认为玻璃态磁性。电阻率呈金属性、Seebeck系数为负，表明自旋无能隙电子结构消失。交换计算显示反位无序引入短的Mn–Mn距离及竞争的Mn–Mn、Mn–Cr反铁磁相互作用，构成磁阻挫的微观基础。

Conclusion: NiMnCrAl的预测自旋无能隙与亚铁磁态对化学无序极为敏感：实际材料中的反位无序破坏了理想的能隙结构并诱发磁阻挫，导致玻璃态磁行为与金属性输运。这一结果凸显了无序控制对于实现和保持自旋无能隙半导体性质的决定性作用。

Abstract: We combine first-principles calculations with structural, magnetic, and transport measurements to investigate the influence of chemical disorder on the quaternary Heusler alloy NiMnCrAl. For the ordered Y-type structure, density-functional-theory calculations predict a ferrimagnetic spin-gapless-semiconducting state with a total magnetic moment of $1.99\,μ_{\mathrm{B}}/\mathrm{f.u.}$ and a mean-field ordering temperature of approximately $1300~\mathrm{K}$. Polycrystalline NiMnCrAl forms in a single average cubic phase; however, combined x-ray diffraction, neutron diffraction, and extended x-ray absorption fine-structure measurements reveal substantial antisite disorder, characterized by predominantly B2-like disorder with additional cross-sublattice mixing. No long-range ferrimagnetic transition is observed up to $700~\mathrm{K}$. Instead, the compound exhibits a broad magnetic anomaly near $37~\mathrm{K}$, frequency-dependent ac susceptibility, slow stretched-exponential relaxation, and field-cooled and zero-field-cooled memory effects, establishing a glassy magnetic state. The electrical resistivity is metallic and the Seebeck coefficient is negative, consistent with the loss of the ideal spin-gapless electronic structure. Exchange-interaction calculations show that antisite disorder introduces short Mn--Mn separations and competing antiferromagnetic Mn--Mn and Mn--Cr interactions, providing a microscopic basis for magnetic frustration. These results demonstrate the strong sensitivity to the predicted spin-gapless and ferrimagnetic states of NiMnCrAl to chemical disorder.

</details>


### [107] [Mechanically induced self-propagating reactions within the realm of mechanochemistry: Fundamentals, overview, in situ monitoring and perspectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22840)
*Imelda Octa Tampubolon,Matej Baláž*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文综述了机械化学中机械诱导自蔓延反应（MSR）这一研究不足的现象，系统梳理了过去二十年的反应体系、影响MSR启动时间的因素，以及原位监测技术带来的机理认识，旨在推动MSR基础研究的复兴。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机械诱导自蔓延反应（MSR）在机械化学领域中是一种前景广阔但研究不足的现象。与渐进的机械化学反应相比，MSR是亚秒级的类燃烧过程，具有产物生成更快、时间效率高、能耗更低等显著优势。尽管机械化学领域处于研究热点，MSR的基础研究仍处于边缘地位。作者希望通过系统回顾当前最新进展，推动MSR基础研究的复兴。

Method: 本文采用系统性综述方法，回顾了过去二十年中被报道以MSR方式进行的反应体系，包括金属-类金属体系（碳化物、硼化物、硅化物、碳氮化物）、金属硫族化合物、金属氧化物及其他特殊反应类型。针对每一类体系简要评述若干典型案例，并附上综合表格。同时，从研磨条件和试剂性质两个角度重新审视了影响MSR进程的因素，分析其与MSR点火时间的正相关或负相关关系。最后总结了原位监测技术为MSR过程提供的机理认识。

Result: 综述系统梳理了过去二十年的MSR反应体系（含金属-类金属体系、金属硫族化合物、金属氧化物等），明确了研磨条件和试剂性质与MSR点火时间之间存在直接或反向的关系，并归纳了原位监测技术对MSR过程机理的最新认识。

Conclusion: MSR作为一种亚秒级、类燃烧的机械化学过程，在时间效率和能耗方面具有明显优势，但基础研究仍显不足。通过系统回顾反应体系、影响因素和原位监测的机理认识，本文旨在催化MSR基础研究的复兴，推动这一领域的深入发展。

Abstract: Mechanically induced self-propagating reaction (MSR) represents a promising but rather underexplored phenomenon within mechanochemistry, a solvent-free alternative to classical solution-based chemistry. In contrast to the gradual mechanochemical reactions, MSRs are sub-second combustive-like processes bringing about substantial benefits such as faster product formation, time efficiency, and lower energy consumption. Despite the fact that the mechanochemical field is in the research spotlight, MSR investigation remains on the periphery. This review aims to catalyze a renaissance in MSR fundamentals investigation by reviewing the current state-of-the-art. This is done via systematically reviewing the reaction systems that were reported to react via a MSR in the past twenty years, such as metal-metalloid ones (carbides, borides, silicides, and carbonitrides), metal chalcogenides, metal oxides, and other specific types of reactions. A couple of case studies are briefly reviewed for each system, and the comprehensive table follows afterward. The review also revisits the factors impacting the MSR course from milling conditions and reagent's properties point of view, showing either a direct or inverse relationship with MSR ignition time. The recent development in the in situ monitoring of mechanochemical reactions allowed to obtain some mechanistic insights into the MSR process, which are summarized in the last chapter.

</details>


### [108] [AlCoCuFeNi High-Entropy Alloy Nanoparticle Melting and Solidification: A Classical Molecular Dynamics Simulation Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22858)
*O. I. Kushnerov*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过经典分子动力学模拟，研究了直径约10 nm的AlCoCuFeNi高熵合金纳米颗粒在三种冷却速率（10^11、10^12、10^13 K/s）下的熔化与凝固行为，发现冷却速率越低，最终结构越趋于结晶态，且熔化与结晶之间存在显著的温度滞后现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高熵合金纳米颗粒因其独特的成分复杂性和潜在应用价值受到关注，但其在纳米尺度下的熔化与凝固机制尚不清晰，尤其是冷却速率对最终相结构（非晶或晶体）的调控作用有待阐明。

Method: 采用经典分子动力学模拟，使用嵌入原子模型（EAM）势，对直径约10 nm的AlCoCuFeNi高熵合金纳米颗粒进行加热熔化与冷却凝固模拟，设定三种冷却速率（10^11、10^12、10^13 K/s）；通过势能随温度的变化表征熔化与结晶行为，并利用自适应共近邻分析（CNA）和径向分布函数（RDF）分析结构演化。

Result: 结果表明，随着冷却速率降低，纳米颗粒的最终结构由非晶态转变为晶态；同时在熔化与结晶过程中观察到显著的温度滞后现象。

Conclusion: 冷却速率是决定AlCoCuFeNi高熵合金纳米颗粒凝固后结构的关键因素：快速冷却有利于形成非晶，慢速冷却则倾向于形成晶体；熔化与结晶之间的温度滞后反映了相变的热力学与动力学特性。

Abstract: The processes of melting and solidification of an AlCoCuFeNi high-entropy alloy nanoparticle with a diameter of about 10 nm were studied by classical molecular dynamics simulation using an embedded atom model potential. Three cooling rates, 10^11, 10^12, and 10^13 K/s, were considered. Melting and crystallization behavior were characterized through the temperature dependence of the potential energy. Structural evolution was analyzed using adaptive common-neighbor analysis (CNA) and radial distribution functions (RDF). The results show that the final structure changes from amorphous to crystalline as the cooling rate decreases. A pronounced temperature hysteresis associated with melting and crystallization was also observed.

</details>


### [109] [Engineering third order absorbing exceptional points in multi-drive cavity magnonics for enhanced sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22875)
*R. D. McKenzie,R. L. Stamps*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文研究一种四模腔磁振系统，由两个耦合谐振器及一对YIG球组成，可通过控制驱动场的相对幅度与相位及YIG球间偶极耦合强度，将系统调谐至三阶吸收型奇异点，适用于高灵敏度探测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共振型奇异点存在本征基矢塌缩（eigenbasis collapse）问题，限制了其在高灵敏度探测中的应用；吸收型奇异点可避免这一缺点，因此探索能够在腔磁振系统中实现并灵活调控高阶吸收型奇异点的方案具有重要意义。

Method: 构建由两个耦合谐振器组成、内各含一个YIG球的四模腔磁振系统，每个谐振器带两个输入输出端口以施加多路驱动场；通过控制YIG球之间的偶极耦合强度，并调节驱动场的相对幅度与相位，将系统调谐至三阶吸收型奇异点。

Result: 证明在该四模腔磁振系统中，通过偶极耦合强度与驱动场幅度、相位的协同调控，可以实现三阶吸收型奇异点，且系统具有相当大的灵活性。

Conclusion: 该系统为高灵敏度探测提供了可行途径，因为吸收型奇异点能够规避共振型奇异点所固有的本征基矢塌缩问题。

Abstract: We consider a four mode cavity magnonics system consisting of two coupled resonators containing a pair of YIG spheres. Each resonator has two input-output ports allowing for the application of multiple drive fields. We show that with control of the dipolar coupling strength between the YIG spheres the relative amplitude and phase of the drive fields can be used to tune the system to third order absorbing exceptional points. The system considered here offers considerable flexibility and may be useful for high sensitivity detection as absorbing exceptional points can avoid the eigenbasis collapse associated with their resonant counterparts.

</details>


### [110] [Temperature Flips the Sign of the Hydrogen Response in Nanogranular Palladium Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22877)
*Niclas Alkazaz,Robert Zierold,Patrick Huber*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 钯纳米颗粒网络中，氢吸收对电阻产生两种相反效应——颗粒内部散射升阻与颗粒膨胀缩缝降阻。本研究证明在宏观多孔硅上通过原子层沉积生长的网络中，操作温度可在固定氢浓度下反转净响应符号，并揭示了温度相关的响应抵消、最小值、退火校准漂移等现象，对定量传感提出热预处理与操控温度要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上把钯纳米颗粒网络对氢的净电阻响应符号视为结构的固有属性，但实际传感中可能出现响应抵消使泄漏难以检出、浓度高于报警阈值却读数偏低等危险情形，亟需厘清温度如何改变颗粒内散射与颗粒间结电阻之间的竞争，从而为可靠的定量氢传感提供依据。

Method: 在宏观多孔硅基底上通过原子层沉积(ALD)生长钯纳米颗粒网络；在不同操作温度(如25–55℃)与200℃退火条件下测量其电阻对氢的响应；建立热力学模型，分析温度升高时颗粒内散射贡献减弱、亚埃级间隙变化主导电阻响应的机制，并预测响应抵消点随温度的变化。

Result: 温度可在固定氢浓度下反转净电阻响应符号；25–40℃之间响应抵消点(零净响应)预测提升逾十倍，跨越已报道的泄漏报警设定值；55℃时响应保持负值但出现最小值，高于设定值的浓度可表现为低于设定值的读数；200℃退火改变校准曲线，并使最低测试浓度下的室温响应转为负值。

Conclusion: 定量氢传感需要明确的退火预处理和能产生可区分信号的特定操作温度；响应极性应视为操作参数而非固定属性。更广泛地，任何颗粒在吸附时发生形变的纳米颗粒网络都可能呈现相同的温度依赖的颗粒内/颗粒间输运竞争，对钯-氢以外的传感器和开关具有启示意义。

Abstract: In nanogranular palladium networks, hydrogen uptake has two opposing effects on electrical resistance. Absorbed hydrogen scatters electrons within the particles, raising their resistance, while particle expansion narrows the gaps between them and lowers the junction resistance. Such networks are widely studied as resistive hydrogen sensors, and the sign of their net response is usually treated as a fixed property of the architecture. In networks grown by atomic layer deposition on macroporous silicon, we show that changing the operating temperature can reverse this sign at a fixed hydrogen concentration. A thermodynamic model indicates that scattering contributes less as temperature rises, allowing sub-angstroem changes in gap width to dominate. Where the two effects cancel, the network absorbs hydrogen yet gives the same reading as in hydrogen-free gas, so a leak could escape detection. Between 25 and 40 degrees Celsius, this cancellation point is predicted to rise over tenfold, crossing reported leak-alarm setpoints. At 55 degrees Celsius, the measured response stays negative but passes through a minimum, so a concentration above a setpoint can mimic one below it. Annealing at 200 degrees Celsius alters the calibration and turns the room-temperature response negative at the lowest concentration tested. Quantitative sensing therefore requires a defined thermal pretreatment and an operating temperature giving distinct signals across the intended concentration range. Response polarity becomes an operating parameter rather than a fixed property. More broadly, any nanoparticle network whose particles deform on sorption may show the same temperature-dependent competition between transport within and between particles, with implications for sensors and switches beyond palladium-hydrogen.

</details>


### [111] [From quasiperiodic three-colour order on a triangular lattice to covalent carbon sheets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22938)
*Chaoyu He*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文研究能否用单张碳片的键（成键端口）来编码晶格的准周期三色着色，构造了黄金、白银、青铜均值三种三角格三色图案，并分析了其局域环境、分子字母表受挫情况、能量学与有限片段的电子光谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索准周期着色图案能否由单一碳片的化学键（最近邻规则与交替成键端口）编码实现，从而为可测试的准周期碳片设计提供结构—能量—光谱关联。

Method: 在三角格上构造黄金均值、白银均值、青铜均值三种三色图案，由最近邻规则从一个颜色确定其余两色；统计各图案的第一壳层环境数（分别为8、9、7）；构建三种“分子字母表”并检验三个相同基元构成的三角形对交替成键端口的受挫；对青铜图案给出四种直接或乙炔基连接的设计，用局域能量模型再生三个周期结构的无限环境频率；对708原子片独立匹配环境计数并与密度泛函结果比较能量；对由两个较小供体初始化的四个周期胞进行弛豫，检查键长均方根变化；拟合一个60度扇区得到最高22140个碳原子的完整六重对称片段；计算13950原子直接连接片的π双重态权重分布。

Result: 黄金、白银、青铜三色图案分别有8、9、7个第一壳层环境；黄金与白银图案中三个相同基元的三角形使三种分子字母表的交替键端口受挫；青铜图案允许四种直接或乙炔基连接设计；三个周期结构在局域能量模型中精确再现无限环境频率；708原子片的预测能量比密度泛函结果每碳低0.295 meV；弛豫后键长均方根变化至多0.00113 Å；13590原子直接连接片中，π双重态79.05%权重集中在仅占7.96%原子的边界壳层，而最低未占据双重态在该处仅0.124%。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一种可测试的基元选择规则，将候选准周期碳片的能量学与有限片段光谱关联起来，表明青铜均值图案因避免受挫而具有更可行的成键设计，并揭示大尺寸片段边界态集中现象。

Abstract: Can the quasiperiodic colouring of a lattice be encoded in the bonds of a single carbon sheet? We construct golden-, silver- and bronze-mean three-colour patterns on a triangular lattice. The positions of one colour determine the other two through a nearest-neighbour rule. The three patterns have eight, nine and seven first-shell environments, respectively. A triangle of three identical motifs frustrates the alternating bond ports of each of the three molecular alphabets in the golden and silver patterns. The bronze pattern admits four designs with direct or ethynylene connections. Three periodic structures reproduce its infinite environment frequencies in a local-energy model. For a 708-atom sheet with independently matched environment counts, the predicted energy lies 0.295 meV per carbon below the density-functional result. Across four periodic cells initialized from two smaller donors, relaxation changes the bond lengths by at most 0.00113 angstroms in root-mean-square terms. Fitting one 60-degree sector to relaxed periodic geometries gives complete sixfold-symmetric fragments with up to 22,140 carbon atoms. In the 13,950-atom directly linked sheet, a $π$ doublet places 79.05% of its weight in a boundary shell comprising 7.96% of the atoms, whereas the lowest unoccupied doublet places only 0.124% there. These calculations connect a testable motif-selection rule to the energetics and finite-patch spectra of candidate quasiperiodic carbon sheets.

</details>


### [112] [Pair distribution function oscillations at electrostatically stabilized nanocrystal surfaces reflect nanocrystal lattice periodicity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22946)
*Vivian R. K. Wall,Andrew Yang,Bryan A. Sanchez Monserrate,Ahhyun Jeong,Christian P. N. Tanner,Joshua Portner,John P. Davis,Tim B. van Driel,Burak Guzelturk,Gihan Kwon,Olaf J. Borkiewicz,David T. Limmer,Dmitri V. Talapin,Karena W. Chapman,Simon J. L. Billinge,Naomi S. Ginsberg*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文采用总X射线散射的双差分对分布函数(ddPDF)技术,首次实验测定了以多价无机配体Sn₂S₆⁴⁻静电稳定的PbS纳米晶表面配体构型,发现配体近似径向排列、呈双齿结合,并揭示ddPDF振荡源于纳米晶-配体间的周期性相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由短链多价无机配体静电稳定的纳米晶表现出丰富的相互作用与相行为,学界认为这些性质取决于离子在纳米晶表面的构型,但此前这类分子尺度的细节从未被实验直接测量。

Method: 作者采用总X射线散射获取双差分对分布函数(ddPDF),测定悬浮于多价电解质中的PbS纳米晶表面Sn₂S₆⁴⁻配体的构型;通过将实测ddPDF与修饰了Sn₂S₆⁴⁻配体的PbS纳米晶模型进行对比,并结合部分对分布函数(partial PDF)计算,以及用强散射Pt原子替代配体的简化模型加以验证。

Result: 实测ddPDF呈现约3 Å波长、延伸约10 Å的振荡。与模型对比表明配体近似径向排列,提示双齿结合;计算的部分PDF显示振荡信号主要由纳米晶-Pb与配体-Sn的相关性主导;简化Pt原子模型证实振荡周期源于纳米晶的晶格常数。

Conclusion: 在解释胶体纳米晶的ddPDF时,纳米晶晶格周期性是一个必须纳入考量的因素。

Abstract: Nanocrystals (NCs) stabilized electrostatically by short, multivalent inorganic ligands exhibit rich interactions and phase behavior believed to hinge on the configuration of ions at the NC surface, yet these molecular details have not previously been measured. Here we use double-difference pair distribution functions (ddPDFs) of total X-ray scattering to determine the configuration of Sn$_2$S$_6^{4-}$ ligands on PbS NCs suspended in multivalent electrolyte. The measured ddPDFs exhibit a $\sim$3-$\mathring{\mathrm{A}}$ wavelength oscillation that extends $\sim$10 $\mathring{\mathrm{A}}$. Comparison with a PbS NC model decorated with Sn$_2$S$_6^{4-}$ ligands indicates ligands are aligned $\sim$radially, suggesting bidentate binding. Calculated partial PDFs show that the oscillatory signal is dominated by nanocrystal-Pb to ligand-Sn correlations. A simplified model that replaces the ligand with a strongly scattering Pt atom near the NC surface confirms the oscillation period in the ddPDF originates from the NC lattice constant. Nanocrystal lattice periodicity therefore merits consideration when interpreting ddPDFs of colloidal NCs.

</details>


### [113] [Spin and Orbital Angular Momentum Textures at Chiral Molecule-Metal Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23029)
*Gyanu P. Kafle,Amos Afugu,Zhen-Fei Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过第一性原理计算，研究手性七螺烯分子吸附对Au(111)表面态自旋与轨道角动量（OAM）纹理的影响，并与清洁Au(111)及非手性晕苯分子体系对比，揭示手性吸附对界面自旋/轨道电子结构的调控及对称性约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手性分子吸附可改变金属表面态的自旋与轨道特性，为理解自旋依赖的界面现象（如手性诱导自旋选择性）提供电子结构基础，但吸附如何具体影响界面自旋与轨道角动量纹理及其对称性关系尚待厘清。

Method: 采用含自旋轨道耦合的第一性原理计算，结合能带展开（band unfolding）和原子投影轨道角动量分析，系统研究手性七螺烯（heptahelicene）分子在Au(111)界面的自旋与OAM纹理，并与清洁Au(111)及非手性晕苯（coronene）/Au体系进行对照。

Result: 分子吸附改变Au衍生表面态在费米能级附近的自旋与OAM矢量取向，并降低其采样OAM幅值；两个七螺烯对映体的纹理经镜像和时间反演对称性相互关联；与非手性晕苯体系的对比表明，部分变化在无非手性环境下同样发生，从而区分了手性特有的贡献。

Conclusion: 该工作阐明了分子吸附如何调控界面自旋与轨道特性，并确立了与手性相关光电子发射现象有关的对称性约束，为理解手性界面自旋电子学提供理论依据。

Abstract: Chiral molecular adsorption can modify the spin and orbital character of metal surface states, providing an electronic-structure basis for interpreting spin-dependent interfacial phenomena. Using first-principles calculations with spin-orbit coupling, band unfolding, and an atom-projected orbital angular momentum (OAM) analysis, we investigate spin and OAM textures at chiral heptahelicene/Au(111) interfaces and compare them with clean Au (111) and an achiral coronene/Au system. Adsorption changes the orientations of the spin and OAM vectors and reduces the sampled OAM magnitudes of Au-derived surface states near the Fermi level. The two heptahelicene enantiomers exhibit textures related by mirror and time-reversal symmetries, whereas the comparison with coronene identifies changes that also occur without molecular chirality. These results clarify how molecular adsorption modifies interfacial spin and orbital character and establish symmetry constraints relevant to chirality-dependent photoemission.

</details>


### [114] [Charge-transfer excitation drives cooperative phonon-assisted exciton self-trapping in NiPS$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23131)
*Long Cheng,Alexander Holm Ohrt,Oliver Nagy,Yu Zhao,Chenyu Wang,Yaxian Wang,Sheng Meng,Guohui Li,Qinying Pan,Xingyu Huang,Sanshui Xiao,Sijie Zhang,Guoqian Liao,Yutong Li,Xuan Luo,Yuping Sun,Kaibo Zheng,Peter Uhd Jepsen,Binbin Zhou*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文利用超宽带光泵-太赫兹探测光谱，实时追踪了范德华反铁磁体 NiPS₃ 中载流子自陷的微观晶格动力学，揭示了先产生未束缚载流子、再演化为关联激子态的非平衡弛豫路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 载流子自陷是强关联量子材料中基本的弛豫途径，但驱动它的微观晶格运动一直难以直接观测。作者希望揭示 NiPS₃ 中激发后载流子定位与晶格动力学的实时关联。

Method: 采用超宽带光泵-太赫兹探测光谱，在跨越配体-金属电荷转移隙的激发条件下，同时实时跟踪载流子局域化与晶格动力学；结合变温测量、模式分辨介电动力学和对称性分析。

Result: 瞬态电导响应以约109飞秒的时间常数猝灭并演化为局域激子态，伴随 Ni-S 光学声子的显著重整化以及2.7 THz瞬态振动模式的出现；2.7和8.0 THz响应被识别为激子自陷的光谱指标，并指向合作双声子机制。

Conclusion: 激发路径是控制强耦合量子材料中载流子局域化与多体弛豫的关键参数；该研究建立了激子自陷的微观晶格图像。

Abstract: Carrier self-trapping is a fundamental relaxation pathway in strongly correlated quantum materials, yet the microscopic lattice motions that drive it have remained largely inaccessible to direct observation. Here we investigate the van der Waals antiferromagnet NiPS$_3$ upon excitation across its ligand-to-metal charge-transfer gap, and reveal a nonequilibrium pathway that initially generates unbound carriers before they evolve into correlated excitonic states. Using ultrabroadband optical-pump terahertz-probe spectroscopy, we simultaneously track carrier localization and the associated lattice dynamics in real time. The transient conductive response quenches with a time constant of $\sim$109 fs and evolves into a localized exciton state, accompanied by pronounced renormalization of Ni-S optical phonons and the emergence of a transient 2.7 THz vibrational mode. Temperature-dependent measurements, together with mode-resolved dielectric dynamics and symmetry analysis, identify the 2.7 and 8.0 THz responses as spectroscopic indicators of exciton self-trapping and point to a cooperative two-phonon mechanism. These findings establish the excitation pathway as a key parameter controlling carrier localization and many-body relaxation in strongly coupled quantum materials.

</details>


### [115] [Localized corrosion resistance of ultrafine-grained austenitic steel 321 obtained by Rotary Swaging: The role of annealing conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23160)
*A. V. Nokhrin,M. K. Chegurov,N. V. Melekhin,D. A. Zotov,V. I. Kopylov,A. N. Sysoev,K. A. Rubtsova*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过旋转锻造获得超细晶奥氏体321钢，研究其在不同退火温度下组织演变（TiC析出、马氏体逆转变、再结晶）与腐蚀性能（一般腐蚀、晶间腐蚀、点蚀）的关系，并确定兼顾强度、塑性与耐蚀性的最佳退火工艺。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究旋转锻造（RS）形成的超细晶奥氏体321钢在退火过程中的组织与性能演变规律，尤其是局部腐蚀（晶间腐蚀与点蚀）抗力的非单调变化机理，以优化其强度—塑性—耐蚀性的综合性能。

Method: 采用旋转锻造制备超细晶奥氏体321钢；在不同温度（450–800°C）退火并表征组织演变（TiC二次析出、马氏体逆转变、再结晶）；通过电化学方法评价一般腐蚀、晶间腐蚀（IGC）与点蚀（PC）抗力，同时测量硬度与显微硬度，并对比棒材表面与心部性能差异。

Result: 450–550°C退火析出二次TiC纳米颗粒；550–650°C发生马氏体向奥氏体的逆转变；700°C开始再结晶。RS后UFG钢晶间腐蚀抗力下降，棒材表面硬度与耐蚀性低于心部；450–550°C退火使显微硬度略升而IGC与PC抗力显著下降；700–800°C再结晶提高PC与IGC抗力。局部腐蚀速率的非单调变化需同时考虑马氏体、δ铁素体、TiC析出及非平衡晶界自由能的变化。

Conclusion: 通过调控退火制度可使超细晶321钢获得强度、塑性与耐蚀性的良好组合；解释其退火过程中局部腐蚀速率非单调变化时，除马氏体与δ铁素体效应外，还须纳入TiC颗粒析出与非平衡晶界自由能变化的影响。

Abstract: The object of this study was austenitic steel 321, which has an ultrafine-grained (UFG) structure formed by Rotary Swaging (RS). RS leads to the formation of a two-phase macroscopically heterogeneous and, at the same time, a microscopically homogeneous UFG structure with an increased content of martensite. It has been shown that secondary TiC nanoparticles form in the annealing at 450-550C, the reverse transformation of martensite into austenite occurs at 550-650C, and recrystallization begins at 700C. Electrochemical studies of the resistance of the UFG steel to general corrosion, intergranular corrosion (IGC), and pitting corrosion (PC) have been carried out. It has been shown that the UFG steel after RS has a reduced IGC resistance. The surface of steel rods after RS has a reduced hardness and corrosion resistance compared to the central part of the rods. It has been shown that in the temperature range of 450-550C, there is a slight increase in the microhardness, and a significant decrease in the IGC and PC resistance of the UFG steel. Recrystallization at 700-800C leads to an increased PC resistance and IGC resistance. Annealing conditions have been determined that ensure a good combination of strength, ductility and corrosion resistance of the UFG steel. It was demonstrated that to explain the non-monotonic variation in the localized corrosion rate of UFG steel during annealing, it is necessary to consider not only the well-known effects of martensite and delta-ferrite, but also the precipitation of TiC particles and the changes in the free energy of non-equilibrium grain boundaries.

</details>


### [116] [Effect of superplastic deformation on the intergranular corrosion resistance of austenitic steel 321 with different types of UFG structure: A case of homogeneous and heterogeneous microstructure obtained by ECAP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23183)
*A. V. Nokhrin,V. I. Kopylov,M. K. Chegurov,S. V. Shotin,C. V. Likhnitskii,N. V. Melekhin,D. A. Zotov,K. A. Rubtsova,O. E. Pirozhnikova,N. N. Berendeev,D. D. Kulagin*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文研究了超塑性对UFG 321L不锈钢组织、硬度与晶间腐蚀抗力的影响，揭示了ECAP循环次数、ECAP温度及退火温度对微观组织非均匀性、超塑性行为及腐蚀性能的调控规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究超塑性变形对超细晶（UFG）321L不锈钢的微观结构、力学性能（硬度、延展性）和晶间腐蚀（IGC）抗力的影响，并厘清微观组织非均匀程度与超塑性行为之间的内在关联。

Method: 通过改变等通道转角挤压（ECAP）循环次数（N）、ECAP温度及退火温度来调控微观组织非均匀程度；随后在低于动态再结晶起始温度的温度区间进行超塑性拉伸试验，并结合硬度测试与晶间腐蚀试验，分析组织、超塑性参数（流变应力、激活能）与IGC抗力之间的演变规律。

Result: 无论组织均匀程度如何，UFG钢均能在低于动态再结晶起始温度下实现超塑性；延展性随试验温度呈非单调变化，在450–600℃出现最小值，800℃时获得最大延伸率（约250%）。超塑性变形受幂律蠕变控制，超塑性后硬度与同温退火样品相当，但IGC抗力降低。异质结构钢延展性和室温拉伸后IGC抗力均较低，其中N=2时IGC抗力最低、N=4时最高；均匀UFG样品室温拉伸后IGC抗力超过粗晶钢。室温拉伸对异质结构钢IGC抗力负面影响最大，对均匀钢影响最小。提高ECAP循环次数可提升750–900℃时的延展性；提高ECAP温度则使延展性和流变应力同时下降。异质结构钢表现出最低的超塑性变形激活能，增加ECAP循环次数或提高ECAP温度均使激活能升高。

Conclusion: 超塑性可在低于动态再结晶起始温度下实现，其变形由幂律蠕变控制，但会降低UFG钢的晶间腐蚀抗力；微观组织非均匀性显著影响延展性、激活能及IGC抗力，均匀组织更有利于室温拉伸后的耐晶间腐蚀性能。

Abstract: The effects of superplasticity (SP) on the structure, hardness, and intergranular corrosion (IGC) resistance of UFG 321L steel are reported. By increasing the number of the ECAP cycles (N) and varying the ECAP temperature, and annealing temperature, the degree of the microstructural heterogeneity was changed. Regardless of the microstructural homogeneity degree, UFG steels demonstrated the possibility of realizing the SP effect at temperatures below the onset temperature of dynamic recrystallization. The dependence of ductility of the UFG steel on the test temperature is non-monotonic with a minimum at 450-600C. The maximum elongation to failure (~250%) was achieved at a SP temperature of 800C. The SP deformation of the UFG steel is controlled by the power law creep. It is shown that the hardness of UFG steel samples after SP is equal than the hardness of UFG steels annealed at the same temperature. It is also shown what SP leads to a decrease in the IGC resistance of the UFG steel. Heterostructured steel have reduced ductility, as well as lower resistance to IGC after tension tests at room temperature (RT). The minimum IGC resistance is ensured at N=2, and the maximum - at N=4. The IGC resistance of homogeneous UFG samples after tension testing exceeds the IGC resistance of coarse-grained steel. Tension test at a RT has the greatest negative effect on the IGC resistance of heterostructured steel, and the least negative effect on the IGC resistance of homogeneous steel. Increasing the number of ECAP cycles enhances the ductility of the UFG steel at 750-900C. Increasing the ECAP temperature results in a simultaneous decrease in ductility and flow stress. Heterostructured steel exhibits minimal values of the activation energy of SP deformation. Increasing the number of ECAP cycles and increasing the ECAP temperature lead to an increase in the activation energy of SP deformation.

</details>

### [117] [Contact-number hyperuniformity at glass close packing in infinite dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22209)
*Harukuni Ikeda*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文用副本理论在高维极限下研究谐波球体系在玻璃近密堆积（GCP）处的接触数涨落，证明结构因子在GCP处小波数下二次消失（接触数超均匀），并在轻微压缩后出现低波数平台，其高度正比于过剩配位数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解无定形堵塞（jamming）端点附近的接触数涨落与超均匀性（hyperuniformity），并解决Gardner不稳定区域如何随空间维度演化、能否沿稳定副本对称破缺分支逼近GCP这一理论难题。

Method: 采用副本理论（replica theory），在高维极限下研究谐波球的玻璃近密堆积。通过将外场耦合到局域接触数，直接从微观相互作用导出总接触数的方差以及波数依赖的结构因子，并分析Gardner不稳定区域随空间维度的收缩行为。

Result: 随空间维度增大，Gardner不稳定区域向jamming收缩，从而可在高维极限下沿稳定的一步副本对称破缺（1RSB）分支逼近GCP。在GCP处，结构因子在小的约化波数下以二次形式消失，表明接触数超均匀；在GCP上方轻微压缩后，结构因子出现低波数平台，其高度正比于过剩配位（excess coordination）。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了接触数超均匀性及其在压缩下破缺的解析描述，揭示了高维极限下GCP可通过稳定的1RSB分支到达，并将宏观压缩性质与微观接触数涨落联系起来。

Abstract: We investigate contact-number fluctuations of harmonic spheres near glass close packing (GCP), the amorphous jamming endpoint reached by compressing an ideal glass. Using replica theory, we show that the Gardner-unstable region shrinks toward jamming as the spatial dimension increases, allowing GCP to be approached along a stable one-step replica symmetry breaking branch in the high-dimensional limit. By coupling an external field to the local contact number, we derive the variance of the total contact number and the wave-number-dependent structure factor directly from the microscopic interactions. At GCP, the structure factor vanishes quadratically at small scaled wave numbers, demonstrating contact-number hyperuniformity. Upon weak compression above GCP, the structure factor develops a low-wave-number plateau whose height is proportional to the excess coordination. These results provide an analytical description of contact-number hyperuniformity and its breakdown upon compression.

</details>


### [118] [Stochastic Dynamics of Domain Walls on a Racetrack: Impact of Inhomogeneity of Magnetic Anisotropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22460)
*Anton V. Hlushchenko,Mykhailo I. Bratchenko,Oksana L. Andrieieva,Kostyantyn I. Polozhiy,Andriy M. Styervoyedov,Igor M. Sokolov,Samudrajit Thapa,Stuart S. P. Parkin,Aleksei V. Chechkin*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文通过二维分形Weierstrass-Mandelbrot函数建模界面各向异性无序，利用微磁学模拟研究了电流驱动磁畴壁在结构无序赛道中的随机输运，发现传输率可由高斯误差函数描述并呈现普适标度行为，并建立了描述畴壁位置涨落的现象学模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 赛道存储中，超薄膜的原子级界面无序会产生空间不均匀的磁各向异性，形成复杂的能量景观，显著影响畴壁运动。然而无序（幅度与空间尺度）如何定量影响电流驱动畴壁输运的随机性及其可靠性极限，尚缺乏统一的理论框架。

Method: 采用二维分形Weierstrass-Mandelbrot函数对各向异性无序进行建模，可独立调控无序幅度与特征空间尺度；通过微磁学模拟研究无热涨落下电流驱动畴壁的输运，以"传输分数"（成功到达探测器的畴壁比例）量化输运行为，分析其随电流密度、无序幅度和空间尺度的依赖关系，并对畴壁轨迹进行统计分析。

Result: 即使在无热涨落条件下，电流驱动畴壁输运仍表现出显著随机性；传输分数依赖关系可由高斯误差函数轮廓描述，且经各自中位数归一化后呈现普适标度行为；畴壁随机位置的方差强烈依赖无序幅度，并可由所提出的现象学模型准确描述。

Conclusion: 该工作为结构无序赛道中随机畴壁输运提供了定量框架，并确立了无序诱导的赛道存储器件可靠性极限。

Abstract: Racetrack memory is a spintronic technology that stores information in current-driven magnetic domain walls (DW), offering a promising route toward non-volatile, high-density, and high-speed memory devices. In ultrathin magnetic films, atomic-scale interface disorder can produce spatially inhomogeneous magnetic anisotropy, creating a complex spatially varying energy landscape that strongly influences DW motion. In this work, we model anisotropy inhomogeneities using a two-dimensional fractal Weierstrass--Mandelbrot function, which provides independent control over the disorder amplitude and characteristic spatial scale. Micromagnetic simulations reveal pronounced stochasticity of current-driven domain-wall transport even in the absence of thermal fluctuations. Transport is quantified by the transmission fraction, defined as the fraction of DWs that successfully reach a detector after propagating through a disordered racetrack. We investigate its dependence on current density, disorder amplitude, and the spatial scale of the anisotropy inhomogeneity. The resulting transmission statistics are described by Gaussian error-function profiles and exhibit a universal scaling behavior upon normalization by the corresponding median values. Statistical analysis of DW trajectories further characterizes the disorder-induced fluctuations of the propagation process. The variance of the random DW positions shows a pronounced dependence on disorder amplitude and is well described by the proposed phenomenological model. These results provide a quantitative framework for describing stochastic DW transport in structurally disordered racetracks and establish disorder-induced reliability limits in racetrack-memory devices.

</details>


### [119] [Connectivity from activity in spiking neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21592)
*Albert J. Wakhloo,Sebastian Mizera*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文证明一类广泛的循环神经网络模型，其突触连接、单细胞响应特性与神经相关性之间满足一组简洁方程；利用高频相关性可以反推出神经元间的有效单突触连接，从而从神经活动中恢复网络连接结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经回路中突触连接与可观测的神经活动（尤其是相关性）之间的定量关系不清晰，作者希望建立统一的理论框架，将网络连接结构、单细胞非线性响应与神经相关性联系起来，从而能够仅凭活动数据推断连接性。

Method: 作者构建了一个处于平稳、异步状态的循环网络建模框架，涵盖广泛的突触滤波器、脉冲非线性以及"随机+结构化"连接。推导出一组方程，将连接性与神经元相关性联系起来，并将相关性自然分解为单突触、双突触及更高阶通路贡献，进而用可实验测量的活动统计量（特别是高频相关性）表达有效单突触连接。

Result: 得到一组简洁方程，相关性可分解为单突触、双突触及高阶通路贡献；并证明可利用高频相关性估计有效单突触连接，从而实现从神经活动恢复网络连接。

Conclusion: 在平稳异步条件下，通过测量神经活动的高频相关性，可以反推出神经元之间的有效单突触连接，从而为从神经数据推断连接组提供理论与方法基础。

Abstract: We show that a wide range of recurrent neural network models obey a simple set of equations tying synaptic connectivity and single-cell response properties to neural correlations. Our modeling framework considers networks in a stationary, asynchronous state and contains a broad class of synaptic filters, spiking nonlinearities, and random-plus-structured connectivity. The resulting equations naturally decompose into contributions from monosynaptic, disynaptic, and higher order paths through the network. We leverage this decomposition to express the effective monosynaptic connection between two neurons in terms of experimentally measurable statistics of the activity, and in particular, their high-frequency correlations. In this way, we show that it is possible to recover network connectivity from neural activity.

</details>
